### [1] [Long range divided differences, clusters, and Graham-positivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10735)
*Hunter Spink,Vasu Tewari*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究A型完全旗簇中与正cluster fan的锥自然相关的环面轨道闭包及其左S_n平移，利用由非交叉交替森林编码的长程divided difference操作计算环面等变度映射，并给出将环面等变同调类展开为Graham正Schubert轮组合的组合算法，应用于环面不变曲线、一般环面轨道闭包及特定Bruhat区间的Richardson簇等。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望推广AJS-Billey公式（原为环面不动点情形），为更一般的环面轨道闭包（包括环面不变曲线、一般环面轨道闭包、以及特定Bruhat区间的Richardson簇）建立组合的Graham正Schubert轮展开，并在Grassmann流形上给出与置换底集上的格路径拟阵相关的分解。

Method: 通过非交叉交替森林编码的长程divided difference操作的复合来计算环面等变度映射；给出将环面等变同调类展开为Graham正Schubert轮组合的组合算法；最后投影到Grassmann流形得到相应的Grassmann Schubert轮分解。

Result: 得到了所有环面不变曲线、一般环面轨道闭包、以及Bruhat区间[w,wc']（其中c'≤s_{n-1}s_{n-2}…s_1）的Richardson簇的组合Graham正Schubert轮展开；并在Grassmann流形上得到与置换底集上的格路径拟阵相关的环面轨道闭包的Graham正Grassmann Schubert轮分解。

Conclusion: 该工作将AJS-Billey公式推广到更广泛的环面轨道闭包类，建立了环面等变度映射与Graham正Schubert轮展开之间的组合算法框架，并在Grassmann流形上给出相应分解。

Abstract: We study torus-orbit closures in the type $A$ complete flag variety naturally associated to cones in the positive cluster fan, together with their left $S_n$-translates. The torus-equivariant degree maps can be computed via composites of long-range divided difference operations encoded by noncrossing alternating forests, and we give combinatorial algorithms to expand the torus-equivariant homology classes into Graham-positive combinations of Schubert cycles. As applications we obtain combinatorial Graham-positive Schubert cycle expansions for all torus-invariant curves (generalizing the AJS-Billey formula for torus-fixed points), generic torus-orbit closures, and Richardson varieties for Bruhat intervals $[w,wc']$ where $c'\le s_{n-1}s_{n-2}\cdots s_1$. Projecting to Grassmannians we also obtain Graham-positive Grassmannian Schubert cycle decompositions of torus-orbit closures associated to lattice path matroids on permuted ground sets.

</details>


### [2] [The Reye geometry inside the 64 lines of the Schur quartic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10751)
*Paweł Nurowski*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Schur四次曲面上的Naskręcki--Pokora $(24_4,32_3)$构型背后的经典几何：在Höhn的$D_4$标记下，对24个根上的对径对合取无不动点商，恰得经典Reye构型；同时确定了完整的64线关联几何的自同构群（阶4608），两条NP构型构成单一轨道，其稳定子阶2304（投影意义下576）；并将64线典范延拓为176线构型，由6条投影等价Schur四次曲面承载，$176=16+16+9\cdot16$，诱导曲面置换群为$S_3\times S_3$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Naskręcki--Pokora构型与Schur四次曲面上的线构型之间存在尚未阐明的经典几何联系；作者希望揭示隐藏于该$(24_4,32_3)$构型中的经典构型（Reye构型），并厘清完整64线关联几何的对称性与延拓结构。

Method: 采用Höhn的$D_4$根标记，对24个根施加对径对合，构造无不动点商以识别Reye构型；通过组合/群论方法计算64线关联几何的自同构群及其轨道与稳定子；并利用6条投影等价Schur四次曲面将线集典范延拓至176线，分析其计数分解$176=16+16+9\cdot16$与诱导的曲面置换群$S_3\times S_3$。

Result: 对径对合诱导的无不动点商恰为经典Reye构型；完整64线关联几何的自同构群阶为4608，两条Naskręcki--Pokora构型处于同一轨道，各自稳定子阶为2304（投影下为576）；64线可典范延拓为176线构型，由6条投影等价Schur四次曲面承载，满足$176=16+16+9\cdot16$，诱导曲面置换群为$S_3\times S_3$。

Conclusion: Schur四次曲面上的Naskręcki--Pokora构型与经典Reye构型通过对径对合商紧密联系；其完整64线几何具有明确的对称群结构，并可自然延拓为176线构型，展现出丰富的对称与计数规律。

Abstract: We identify the classical geometry hidden in the Naskręcki--Pokora $(24_4,32_3)$ configuration on the Schur quartic. In Höhn's $D_4$ labelling, the antipodal involution on the $24$ roots induces a fixed-point-free quotient of the incidence configuration, and this quotient is precisely the classical Reye configuration. We also determine the symmetry of the complete $64$-line incidence geometry: its automorphism group has order $4608$, the two Naskręcki--Pokora configurations form a single orbit, and the stabilizer of either has order $2304$ (projectively, $576$). Finally, the $64$ lines extend canonically to a $176$-line arrangement carried by six projectively equivalent Schur quartics, with $176=16+16+9\cdot16$ and induced surface permutation group $S_3\times S_3$.

</details>


### [3] [Torelli theorem for the product of moduli spaces of vector bundles over a curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10764)
*Indranil Biswas,Arijit Dey,Anubhab Pahari*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了光滑射影曲线（亏格 g≥4）上半稳定向量丛模空间之积的 Torelli 型定理，并计算了其自同构群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的 Torelli 定理（曲线由其 Jacobian 决定）推广到半稳定向量丛模空间（及模栈）的乘积情形，刻画这些几何对象在多大程度上决定了底曲线，并确定其自同构群。

Method: 利用关于 Picard 秩为一且 Picard 群离散的正规射影簇之积的分解定理（decomposition theorem），将乘积结构归约到各因子的结构性质，从而建立 Torelli 型结论。

Result: 对亏格 g≥4 的光滑射影曲线上半稳定向量丛模空间之积证明了 Torelli 型定理，并对相应的半稳定向量丛模栈之积给出了类似结论；同时确定了这些模空间之积与模栈之积的自同构群。

Conclusion: 乘积的模空间/模栈在 Torelli 意义下决定底曲线，其自同构群可被完全计算；核心工具是 Picard 秩一且 Picard 群离散的正规射影簇之积的分解定理。

Abstract: We prove a Torelli-type theorem for a product of the moduli spaces of semistable vector bundles over smooth projective curves of genus $g\,\ge\, 4$. A similar result is proved for the product of the moduli stacks of semistable vector bundles. This is proved using a decomposition theorem for the product of the normal projective varieties with Picard rank one and discrete Picard group. As an application, we compute the automorphism group of the product of the moduli spaces and moduli stacks.

</details>


### [4] [Perverse Euler Characteristics of Hermitian Locally Symmetric Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10946)
*Mert Akdenizli,Quoc-Anh Tran*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明了非紧型有限体积局部Hermite对称空间的perverse Euler示性数非负，并刻画了全支撑情形的严格性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究局部Hermite对称空间（及各类模空间）上perverse Euler示性数的符号性质，这一问题与代数几何中的正性现象及模空间的上同调性质密切相关。

Method: 证明光滑环面紧化的对数余切丛具有nef性；将该正性结果与perverse层Euler示性数的正性判别准则相结合，推出所需结论。

Result: 获得非紧型有限体积局部Hermite对称空间的perverse Euler示性数非负性；对具有全支撑的perverse层，相应不等式严格成立。

Conclusion: 作为应用，得到若干模空间上perverse Euler示性数的非负性结果。

Abstract: We prove that finite-volume locally Hermitian symmetric spaces of noncompact type have nonnegative perverse Euler characteristics. To show this, we obtain a nefness result for the logarithmic cotangent bundle of a smooth toroidal compactification. Combining this with a positivity criterion for Euler characteristics of perverse sheaves, we deduce the nonnegativity result. We further prove that the inequality is strict for perverse sheaves with full support. As applications, we get nonnegativity results for perverse Euler characteristics on various moduli spaces.

</details>


### [5] [Coupled Pklt Tuples and Varieties of Pklt Type](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11107)
*Donghyeon Kim,Dae-Won Lee*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文引入与投影klt对上的伪有效除子元组相关的渐近乘子理想层与对数典范阈值，证明了耦合potentially klt元组的阈值由拟单项赋值计算，并给出potentially klt型簇上big除子的双有理Zariski分解、multisection环的有限生成及Mori dream space判定准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将乘子理想层、对数典范阈值、Zariski分解与Mori dream space等经典工具与判据推广到potentially klt型簇及除子元组的情形，拓展双有理几何中奇点与除子的研究框架。

Method: 引入渐近乘子理想层与对数典范阈值，定义耦合的potentially klt元组；利用拟单项赋值刻画其阈值，并通过双有理几何方法构造big除子的Zariski分解。

Result: 耦合potentially klt元组的阈值可由拟单项赋值计算；potentially klt型簇上每个big除子具有正部分半丰的Zariski分解；big除子的multisection环有限生成；给出potentially klt型簇为Mori dream space的判定准则。

Conclusion: 该研究将乘子理想层与Mori dream space理论推广至potentially klt型簇与除子元组，为相关双有理几何问题提供新的刻画与判据。

Abstract: We introduce asymptotic multiplier ideal sheaves and log canonical thresholds associated with tuples of pseudoeffective divisors on a projective klt pair. We prove that the threshold of a coupled potentially klt tuple is computed by a quasi-monomial valuation. For varieties of potentially klt type, we prove that every big divisor admits a birational Zariski decomposition with semiample positive part. We also prove finite generation of multisection rings of big divisors and give a criterion for a variety of potentially klt type to be a Mori dream space.

</details>


### [6] [Graded Betti numbers of general curves of large degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11161)
*JeongDon Lee,Li Li,Jinhyung Park*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文在特定条件下给出了曲线射影嵌入的Koszul上同调群分级Betti数的显式闭式公式，并确定了通用曲线的完整Betti表及Boij–Söderberg系数的渐近行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当线丛次数充分大时，K_{p,q}(C,L)的消失与非消失性已被确定，但分级Betti数κ_{p,q}(C,L)的具体值仍基本未知，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 设C为亏格g、gonality为k的光滑射影复曲线，L为very ample线丛。在Brill–Noether轨迹W_k^1(C)具有预期维数且H^1(C, L⊗ω_C^{-1})=0的条件下，推导所有分级Betti数κ_{p,q}(C,L)的显式闭式公式。

Result: 得到全部κ_{p,q}(C,L)的闭式公式；确定当deg L ≥ 4g-3，或deg L ≥ 3g-3且L为一般线丛时通用曲线的完整Betti表；显式计算支配渐近纯性的section环R(C,L)的Boij–Söderberg系数。

Conclusion: 剩余系数呈现最终单调性：超椭圆曲线情形递减，在相关Brill–Noether轨迹的自然一般既约性假设下递增。

Abstract: Let $C$ be a smooth projective complex curve of genus $g$ and gonality $k$, and $L$ be a very ample line bundle on $C$. When $L$ has sufficiently large degree, the vanishing and nonvanishing of the Koszul cohomology groups $K_{p,q}(C,L)$ have been determined previously, but the exact values of the graded Betti numbers $κ_{p,q}(C, L)$ remain largely unknown. In this paper, we give explicit closed formulas for all graded Betti numbers $κ_{p,q}(C, L)$ when the Brill--Noether locus $W_k^1(C)$ has the expected dimension and $H^1(C, L \otimes ω_C^{-1})=0$. Consequently, we determine the complete Betti table for a general curve when $°L \geq 4g-3$ or when $°L \geq 3g-3$ and $L$ is general. We also explicitly compute the Boij--Söderberg coefficient of the section ring $R(C, L)$ governing asymptotic purity, and show eventual monotonicity of the remaining coefficients: they decrease for hyperelliptic curves and increase under a natural generic reducedness assumption on the relevant Brill--Noether loci.

</details>


### [7] [Divisibility and torsion in higher Chow groups over arithmetic fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11178)
*Toshiro Hiranouchi,Rin Sugiyama*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 研究高维周群 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 的阿贝尔群结构：在 $i\ge \mathrm{cd}_l(F)$ 时给出可除性与无挠性结果；对光滑真且几何不可约的 $X$，刻画前推映射 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)\to CH^i(F,j)$ 的核，并应用于有限域、局部域与整体域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维周群（higher Chow groups）虽已有丰富理论，但其阿贝尔群结构（可除性、挠性、挠部分）在一般情形下仍不清楚，尤其是在低余维（大 $i$）范围内；作者希望通过 $l$-上同调维数条件控制 $l$-主挠，并理解几何基变换（前推）映射的核，从而将抽象结果落地到经典数论域类上。

Method: 设 $X$ 是域 $F$ 上维数 $d$ 的光滑概型，取 $l\ne \mathrm{char}(F)$。利用 $l$-进上同调与周群的比较（如 étale/顺变上同调、Bloch–Kato 等高权重工具），在 $i\ge \mathrm{cd}_l(F)$ 的范围内分析 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 的 $l$-可除性与无挠性；对光滑真且几何不可约 $X$，通过前推 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)\to CH^i(F,j)$ 及 $l$-进实现的权重/伽罗瓦作用，确定其核。

Result: 对任意素数 $l\ne \mathrm{char}(F)$，若 $i\ge \mathrm{cd}_l(F)$，证明了 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$ 的相应 $l$-可除性与无挠性结论；当 $X$ 光滑真且几何不可约时，给出了前推映射 $CH^{d+i}(X,j)\to CH^i(F,j)$ 的核的刻画；并将这些结果应用于有限域、局部域与整体域。

Conclusion: 该工作表明高维周群的阿贝尔群结构在 $i\ge \mathrm{cd}_l(F)$ 的范围内受域的上同调维数严格控制，前推核可由几何与算术不变量描述，并在有限域、局部域、整体域等经典情形中获得具体结论，为后续研究周群的挠性与基变换提供框架。

Abstract: Let $X$ be a smooth scheme of dimension $d$ over a field $F$. We study the abelian-group structure of the higher Chow groups $CH^{d+i}(X,j)$. For a prime $l$ different from the characteristic of $F$, we prove divisibility and torsion-freeness results when $i\ge$ the $l$-cohomological dimension of $F$. If $X$ is smooth proper and geometrically irreducible, we also study the kernel of the push-forward $CH^{d+i}(X,j)\to CH^i(F,j)$. We apply these results to finite fields, local fields, and global fields.

</details>


### [8] [Openness of local properties in flat families of superschemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11287)
*Ibrahim Ahmad*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文证明平坦真态超概形 fibrations 中纤维的正规性、GFRR、正则性和 Cohen-Macaulay 性是开条件，并由此得到从特殊纤维到一般纤维的性质提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jankowski 最近借助 Serre 判据引入了超概形的正规性与既约性（generic fermionic regularity 与 reducedness）。自然的问题是：这些超几何性质在族（morphism）中是否具有开性，以及是否可沿退化从特殊纤维提升到一般纤维。

Method: 考虑平坦、真态态射 f:X→Y，其中 X 是超概形，Y 是特征 0 域上有限型的 Dedekind 概形。证明纤维 X_y 为 normal、GFRR、regular、Cohen-Macaulay 的 y 集合在 Y 中开。并特化到 A_C^{1|0} 上的退化情形。

Result: 证明了 {y∈Y : X_y 是 normal / GFRR / regular / Cohen-Macaulay} 在 Y 中为开集。特别地，对于 A_C^{1|0} 上的退化，这些性质可从特殊纤维提升到一般纤维。

Conclusion: 超概形中正规性、GFRR、正则性与 Cohen-Macaulay 性满足开性定理与退化提升性质，与非超代数几何中相应结果平行。

Abstract: Recently, Jankowski introduced the Serre criteria for superschemes to define the super versions of normality and reducedness (called generic fermionic regularity and reducedness). We show that for a flat, proper morphism $f:X\rightarrow Y$, where $X$ is a superscheme and $Y$ is a Dedekind scheme of finite type over a field of characteristic $0$, the set of $y\in Y$ for which the fibers $X_y$ are normal, GFRR, regular, and Cohen-Macaulay is open in $Y$. In particular, if one has a degeneration over $\mathbb{A}_{\mathbb{C}}^{1|0}$, this shows that these properties can be lifted from the special fiber to the generic one.

</details>


### [9] [Generic vanishing conjecture for divisors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11377)
*Fanjun Meng*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文受Pareschi–Popa一般消没猜想的启发，证明了该猜想在余维1情形下的一个类似结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pareschi与Popa提出的一般消没猜想刻画了主极化阿贝尔簇上余维大于1、代表极小上同调类的子簇之存在性与其理想层经主极化扭变后的正性之间的关系。作者希望在此框架下探索余维1的对应情形。

Method: 类比Pareschi–Popa一般消没猜想的表述，研究主极化阿贝尔簇中余维1子簇（即除子）的极小上同调类性质及其理想层经主极化扭变后的正性，并给出相应猜想的证明。

Result: 证明了Pareschi–Popa一般消没猜想的一个余维1类似命题。

Conclusion: 一般消没猜想在余维1情形下成立（或其类似形式成立），推广了原有关于高余维子簇的框架。

Abstract: Generic vanishing conjecture proposed by Pareschi and Popa is about the relationship between the existence of subvarieties of codimension $>1$ of principally polarized abelian varieties representing minimal cohomology class and the positivity of their ideal sheaves twisted by the principal polarization. Inspired by their formulation, we prove a codimension $1$ analog of generic vanishing conjecture.

</details>


### [10] [Campana Rational Connectedness and Weak Approximation of Del Pezzo Orbifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11533)
*Saptarshi Dandapat*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 证明次数小于7的 del Pezzo 轨道簇、以及与不可约边界相关的高次 del Pezzo 轨道簇是强 Campana 单直纹的，从而推出其为 Campana 有理连通；给出 Campana 猜想在二维情形下的非环面例子，并得到 Campana 纤维的弱逼近；附录分类了次数为6、7、8且具不可约边界的 del Pezzo 轨道簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Campana 猜想在二维情形缺乏非环面（non-toric）例子，且该猜想的某种形式与弱逼近定理相关，需要构造相应例子并验证其性质，以支持该猜想并推导弱逼近结论。

Method: 对 del Pezzo 轨道簇按度数分情形研究：处理次数小于7的情形，以及高次但边界不可约的情形；并借助附录中对次数6、7、8且具不可约边界的 del Pezzo 轨道簇的分类来推进论证。

We find a clear hierarchy: amplitudes with a single massive external state vanish, those with exactly two are independent of the nonlinear bimetric parameters, and nonlinear ghost-free parameter dependence first appears with three massive external states. For nonstandard boundary conditions, the modified symplectic structure yields new expressions for the entropy formula of black holes.

We give an explicit reversible quantum circuit for the block-inversion step and validate it in a register-level simulator. We also identify the exact structural boundary where the method stops and propose a candidate construction in the surviving regime under a new, clearly stated security assumption. The constructive results are supported by reproducible software artifacts and experiments.

Abstract: We introduce $Krylov$ $edge$ $spectroscopy$, a many-body operator-space protocol for detecting and classifying one-dimensional symmetry-protected topological phases from local boundary dynamics. This transition occurs precisely at the bulk gap closing, and its localization-length exponent coincides with the Ising correlation-length exponent. Through operator Krylov dynamics, our work turns local boundary evolution into a direct spectroscopy of many-body topology.

Abstract: We construct Floquet codes from the Majorana XYZ subsystem code with local realizations both in qubits and directly in microscopic Majorana modes with lattice size $L\times L$. For $L=4n$, by contrast, one Pauli of the second logical qubit remains fixed throughout the cycle, while every representative of its conjugate must evolve through the measurement cycle. This distinction follows from a parity-dependent algebraic obstruction and disappears when the cycle is reduced to two steps, which restores a fully static logical pair. Thus, the same encoded logical degree of freedom can be switched between static and partially dynamical forms by the measurement schedule. Abstract: Leakage, occurring when a qubit undetectably exits the computational subspace, poses a significant challenge for quantum error correction by inducing correlated errors in space and time. These correlations reduce both the code threshold and the effective code distance. To address this challenge, we introduce two zero-overhead walking circuits for the honeycomb Floquet code (hFC), termed the swirling and sliding circuits, which periodically remove leakage while dynamically protecting logical qubits via a schedule of anticommuting two-body measurements. Unlike previous dynamic circuits for the hFC, ours preserves the distance to Pauli errors. We further study their performance under leakage and find that, for two leakage noise models, they may correct as many leakage errors as Pauli errors, improving on the more widely known walking surface code. Through numerical simulation, we observe that finite-error-rate performance under leakage is strongly influenced by entropic effects, producing a pronounced waterfall regime in which the logical error rate decreases much more rapidly with physical error rate than the expected distance-limited scaling. In this regime, even when asymptotic predictions suggest otherwise, the hFC can outperform the same-distance walking surface code in error rates and sub-threshold logical error scaling.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Clifford层级是一类可通过门传态容错执行的量子门序列，其结构备受关注。Zeng-Chen-Chuang猜想所有层级门都是广义半Clifford门（形式为C1 Π D C2），Beigi-Shor证明了第三层门满足该性质。本文旨在检验该猜想在更高层是否成立。

Abstract: The Clifford hierarchy is a nested sequence of sets of quantum gates that can be fault-tolerantly performed using gate teleportation within standard quantum error correction schemes. The importance of these gates has motivated numerous studies of their structure. Zeng-Chen-Chuang conjectured in 2007 that all hierarchy gates are generalised semi-Clifford, i.e. take the form $C_1 ΠD C_2$ for Clifford gates $C_1, C_2$, a permutation gate $Π$, and a diagonal gate $D$; Beigi-Shor proved in 2008 that this holds for all third-level gates.
  We construct a five-qubit gate that is in the fifth level of the Clifford hierarchy but is not generalised semi-Clifford. Rather than simply present and verify our counterexample to the generalised semi-Clifford conjecture, we show how its form can be deduced. Our counterexample also demonstrates that the Clifford hierarchy is not closed under inverses.

</details>

Abstract: In the context of the QGEM (Quantum Gravity-induced Entanglement of Masses) experiment, we consider two adjacent matter-wave interferometers in linear and parallel configurations that interact solely via gravity. If gravity were quantum, then the two matter-wave interferometers would become entangled via the virtual excitation of the massless graviton. In this paper, we consider witnessing this entanglement by considering a generic experimental scenario where the two interferometers are subject to different global phases and different decoherence rates. In this context, we show that the individual global phases do not affect the witness, discuss common and differential decoherence modes, and perform the parameter search optimal for different masses. We provide a mathematical framework for these asymmetric decoherence rates and then search for parameters that determine the entanglement witness. We have kept the inter-separation distance between the two closest superpositions of the interferometers' masses fixed while varying the experimental time from $τ=0.1$ s to $τ=1$ s. Finishing the experiment at $ τ=0.1$ s has many advantages from the point of view of protecting the experiment from random acceleration noise. However, witnessing the entanglement also suffers from $\langle W\rangle \sim -{\cal O}(10^{-2})$ for $m=10^{-14}$~kg, for decoherence rate in the ranges of ${\cal O}(10^{-1}-1)$~Hz for $τ=0.1$ s experiment. However, as we show, increasing the mass of the matter-wave interferometer may improve the witness considerably.

Abstract: We develop a quantum approach to spectral feature extraction from the density of states (DOS) of a problem-dependent Hamiltonian, and apply it to machine learning on signed graphs. We show that these moments count signed closed walks, are switching-invariant, and are size-free by construction. Abstract: Much work has been done in the last two decades on the topic of quantum state transfer in a quantum spin network. One can model such a system of interacting qubits using an undirected graph, and studying vertex-to-vertex dynamics. This setup has recently been relaxed to allow for dynamics between linear combinations of two vertex states, i.e.\ from $\mathbf u = \mathbf e_a + s \mathbf e_b$ to $\mathbf μ=\mathbf e_α + r \mathbf e_β$, where $r=s$ is either $-1$ (which corresponds to pair state transfer) or $+1$ (which corresponds to plus state transfer), or more recently $r=s$ is taken to be any real number (which corresponds to $s$-pair state transfer).
  Here, we broaden the investigation of $s$-pair state transfer to \textit{perfect $(s,r)$-state transfer}, which is perfect state transfer from $\mathbf u = \mathbf e_a + s \mathbf e_b$ to $\mathbf μ=\mathbf e_α + r \mathbf e_β$ (up to some dilation) where $r,s\in \mathbb C$. We identify infinite families of graphs with perfect $(s,r)$-state transfer and provide characterizations of cases when $|r|= |s|$ and when $|r|\neq |s|$, showing situations when the degree of entanglement between vertex states is preserved, and when it is not preserved. The latter is particularly important as it represents perfect state transfer from an entangled pair of qubits to another one where the degree of entanglement need not be the same\mdash in fact, it can be set up so as to ``boost'' (increase) entanglement. We provide an algorithm that finds the vector with two nonzero entries that maximizes the fidelity of transfer for a fixed time $t$ starting from a given $s$-pair state $\mathbf u$. Finally, we provide a sensitivity analysis, with respect to readout time errors, of perfect $(s,r)$-state transfer.

Abstract: We present Tomography-NMR, an open-source Python package that reconstructs quantum density matrices from spectroscopic measurement data. The package implements a complete analysis pipeline for two-qubit quantum state tomography based on the product operator formalism: raw time-domain signals are Fourier-transformed into frequency-domain spectra, spectral peak intensities are mapped to expansion coefficients of the density matrix, and the full quantum state is reconstructed. Three integration methods are provided for different use cases: direct peak height measurement and fixed-parameter numerical integration require no theoretical reference and are suited to unknown states, achieving fidelities of approximately 98\% on a benchmark set of known states, while a systematic parameter optimization against a known target state achieves reconstruction fidelities exceeding 99\% for the same benchmark states. While the detailed theoretical framework for quantum state tomography is well established, the practical procedures for extracting density matrices from experimental spectra are often inadequately documented in the literature and obscured within proprietary software. This package addresses that gap by providing a fully transparent, reproducible implementation of every analysis step, from spectral preprocessing to density matrix visualization. The software has been validated on experimentally prepared two-qubit states measured via nuclear magnetic resonance (NMR) spectroscopy of coupled $^{31}$P nuclei. Average reconstruction fidelities range from 0.975 to 0.995 across a benchmark set of 20 two-qubit states, including the computational basis states, Bell states, and the outputs of three fundamental quantum gates (CNOT, H, and T). Although developed for NMR, the modular architecture facilitates adaptation to other spectroscopic platforms and alternative measurement protocols.

</details>

Abstract: Quantifying properties of quantum states through the limits of their manipulation is a central goal of quantum resource theories. For symmetry breaking, the quantum geometric tensor governs asymptotic pure-state conversion, but a complete characterization for general mixed states has remained elusive.

Specifically, we establish a single-letter formula for the optimal conversion rate between arbitrary states, with vanishing trace-distance error, in the resource theory of asymmetry. The rate is determined by a one-parameter family of quantum Fisher information (QFI) matrices that interpolates between the symmetric- and right-logarithmic-derivative QFIs. No state-independent finite subset of this family suffices in general, even for $U(1)$ symmetry, revealing a qualitative distinction from pure-state conversion. Our formula further yields an exact formula for pure-state distillation rates in terms of the generalized quantum geometric tensor, characterizes asymptotically reversible interconversion, and identifies bound asymmetry for quantum clocks. Complementarity among different members of the QFI family also uncovers an activation mechanism for quantum clocks.

Second, we characterize convertibility between quantum Gaussian shift models in terms of the same one-parameter family of QFIs. Together, these results provide an operational characterization of symmetry breaking for general quantum states in the i.i.d. asymptotic regime and reveal how distinct QFI constraints give rise to irreversibility and activation.

Abstract: How much memory is needed to retain the quantum speedup for collision finding? For a uniformly random function $f:[N]\to [N]$, the BHT algorithm finds a collision using $O(N^{1/3})$ queries and a quantumly accessible classical table containing $O(N^{1/3})$ input-output pairs, whereas a logarithmic-space Grover search uses $O(\sqrt N)$ queries. Determining the optimal query-space tradeoff between these extremes remains a major open problem.
  We resolve this equation within the class of label-symmetric algorithms, which treat the function $f$'s output labels as interchangeable. We prove that such algorithm that makes $T$ queries, uses $S$ qubits, and finds a collision in a uniformly random function $f:[M]\to [N]$ with constant probability satisfies $$T=Ω(N^{1/3}) \qquad\text{and}\qquad T^2S=Ω(N\log N).$$ For the setting where $M=N$, these bounds are matched by a space-efficient implementation of the BHT algorithm. As a consequence of our tradeoff, any label-symmetric algorithm for the search version of Element Distinctness on $f: [n] \to [n^2]$ must satisfy $$T=Ω(n^{2/3}) \qquad\text{and}\qquad T^2S=Ω(n^2\log n),$$ matching Ambainis's quantum walk. Thus, both tradeoffs are optimal within the class of label-symmetric algorithms.

The compressed oracle records the information learned by the algorithm in an evolving superposition of databases. Using label symmetry and representation theory, we show that an algorithm using $S$ qubits can effectively retain information about only $O(S/\log N)$ collision-free database entries. Substituting this estimate into the compressed oracle technique yields the stated tradeoffs.

Result: 证明了在独立伯努利Pauli噪声下Rényi-n相干信息关于n∈Z+非减，该结论同样覆盖经典线性码与独立探测器差错模型；对任意随机Pauli噪声，Rényi-n相干信息可通过症候后选择获得操作解释，其饱和等价于后选择信道的渐近完美恢复；此外，Rényi-n相干信息还给出任意症候条件恢复后所能达到的普通相干信息的上界。

Conclusion: Rényi相干信息在稳定子码情形下既恢复了关于Rényi指标的单调性，又获得了基于症候后选择的操作意义，从而在混合态物相与可解码性转变研究中更具可靠性。

Abstract: Rényi coherent information, a computable proxy for the von Neumann coherent information, is widely used to study mixed-state phases of matter and decodability transitions in noisy quantum error-correcting codes. However, being a difference of two Rényi entropies, it need not be monotonic in the Rényi index, and lacks the operational meaning of its von Neumann counterpart. Here we address both issues for stabilizer codes. First, for Pauli noise generated by independent Bernoulli events, we prove that the Rényi-$n$ coherent information is nondecreasing in $n \in \mathbb{Z}^+$. This follows from a general theorem: if independent random bits are mapped linearly to a fine label $T$ and a coarse label $C$, then the Rényi entropy difference $H_n(C)-H_n(T)$ is nondecreasing in $n$. For stabilizer codes, $T$ is the joint syndrome--logical class and $C$ is the syndrome, and the difference is the Rényi-$n$ coherent information up to a constant. The same theorem covers classical linear codes and independent detector error models. Second, for arbitrary stochastic Pauli noise, we give the Rényi-$n$ coherent information an operational meaning via postselection on matching syndromes between one data block and $n-1$ auxiliary blocks. We determine when this defines a quantum channel and show that saturation of the Rényi-$n$ coherent information is equivalent to asymptotically perfect recovery of the postselected channel. Moreover, the Rényi-$n$ coherent information also upper-bounds the ordinary coherent information achievable after any syndrome-conditioned recovery.

Abstract: In this work, we analyze the average-case hardness of approximation for the Quantum Hypergraph Max-Cut problem using the theoretical framework of the Quantum Overlap Gap Property (QOGP). We establish two main results. Our first result applies to a wide class of stable quantum algorithms, satisfying a Lipschitz property with respect to the quantum Wasserstein distance of order $2$. We show a weak hardness result, demonstrating that for any Lipschitz constant $L$, there is some $k$ such that $L$-stable algorithms cannot approximate the optimal solution to Quantum Hypergraph Max-Cut on $k$-uniform hypergraphs in the average case. Additionally, we establish a strong hardness result where $k$ is independent of $L$, but only for a more restricted class of local quantum algorithms defined using the quantum Wasserstein distance of order $\infty$. We apply these results to establish concrete depth lower bounds for popular quantum algorithms for preparing near-optimal states for this problem.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 对ZX表面码，X-memory阈值随偏置单调上升，而Z-memory阈值下降并趋于饱和；对ZY表面码，Y-memory阈值在各偏置值下几乎恒定；两种码的Z-memory阈值在不确定度内一致。加入XX串扰使Z-memory阈值降低（超出拟合不确定度），而X-memory阈值基本不受影响。CNOT排序会重新分配两个逻辑存储之间的阈值性能。

Abstract: Studying the threshold behavior of surface codes under biased noise models is an active area of research. Previous work Tuckett et al. (2018), using an optimal tensor-network decoder, demonstrated that replacing $Z$-type stabilizers with $Y$-type stabilizers significantly improves the surface code threshold under code-capacity level dephasing noise. In this work, we construct and study a $ZY$ surface code by replacing the $X$-type stabilizers with $Y$-type stabilizers. We compare it with the standard $ZX$ surface code under circuit-level Pauli-$X$ biased noise, with and without an additional gate-based $XX$ crosstalk noise. We find that for the $ZX$ surface code, the $X$-memory threshold increases monotonically with bias while the $Z$-memory threshold decreases and saturates. For the $ZY$ surface code, the $Y$-memory threshold is nearly constant across all bias values. The $Z$-memory thresholds of the $ZX$ and $ZY$ codes are consistent within the uncertainty. Adding $XX$ crosstalk reduces the $Z$-memory threshold beyond the fitting uncertainty while leaving the $X$ memory threshold largely unaffected. The choice of CNOT ordering redistributes threshold performance between the two logical memories. Our work extends prior observations from code-capacity level noise to circuit-level noise. It also indicates the need for decoders capable of jointly reasoning over correlated syndrome information so that tailored stabilizer structures could be fully utilized for quantum error correction.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 作者针对复制转移动力学（replicated transfer dynamics）发展了一种固定键维的矩阵乘积态（MPS）方法，在超越精确传播计算能力的同时保留了转变的有限尺寸特征。

Result: 在超过以往精确研究的系统尺寸下，作者复现了一维淬火无序无阈值的预测，获得了更尖锐的退火全连接临界点，并解析出二维方格与重六角（heavy-hex）电路中依赖架构的有限深度阈值。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了复制张量网络动力学作为探测大规模及高维含噪电路中误差缓解阈值的实用工具。

Abstract: Quantum error mitigation relies on accurate noise characterization, but mismatches between the actual and characterized noise can be amplified and drive a sharp threshold between successful and failed mitigation. In random circuits, this threshold maps onto a random-field Ising transition, but previous exact numerics were limited to small one-dimensional and all-to-all systems, leaving explicit two-dimensional architectures unresolved. We develop a fixed-bond-dimension matrix-product-state method for the replicated transfer dynamics that extends threshold calculations beyond exact propagation while retaining the finite-size signatures of the transition. At system sizes beyond previous exact studies, we recover the predicted absence of a threshold for quenched disorder in 1D, obtain a sharper annealed all-to-all critical point, and resolve architecture-dependent finite-depth thresholds in 2D square and heavy-hex circuits. These results establish replicated tensor-network dynamics as a practical tool for probing error-mitigation thresholds in large and higher-dimensional noisy circuits.

</details>

Result: 当一侧为量子比特时，普通边缘词族决定部分转置谱，从而判定PPT性质；在2⊗2和2⊗3系统中给出完全可分性判据。两量子比特时，有限子族可分离局域酉轨道，有限扩张生成多项式不变环。量子比特-量子三维时，存在满秩、局部极大混合、所有普通边缘词不变量相同但算子-Schmidt秩不同的态对，可由一个四次关联不变量分开。两维度均≥3时，局部极大混合扇区出现由可分态与NPT纠缠态构成的同谱对。因此普通Bargmann代数等于完整局域酉不变量环当且仅当两个子系统都是量子比特。

Abstract: Bargmann invariants constructed from a bipartite density operator and its two lifted marginals are polynomial invariants of local-unitary conjugation. We determine the precise information encoded in these invariants. Whenever one subsystem is a qubit, the ordinary marginal-word family determines the full spectrum of the partial transpose and hence decides whether the state has the positive-partial-transpose property. In $2\otimes2$ and $2\otimes3$ systems, this yields complete separability criteria. For the two-qubit system, a finite subfamily additionally separates local-unitary orbits, and a finite extension generates the polynomial invariant ring. These three tasks already diverge for qubit-qutrit states: we exhibit full-rank, locally maximally mixed states that agree on all ordinary marginal-word invariants yet have different operator-Schmidt ranks, together with a quartic correlation invariant that separates them. When both local dimensions are at least three, the analogous collapse on the locally maximally mixed sector produces isospectral pairs consisting of one separable state and one entangled state with negative partial transpose. The ordinary Bargmann algebra therefore coincides with the full local unitary invariant ring if and only if both subsystems are qubits. The missing data are geometric: they encode the placement of global eigenspaces relative to the tensor-product decomposition

### [72] [Distributed variational quantum computing with deterministic entanglement tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10932)
*Ilhwan Kim,Yong-Su Kim,Kwang Jo Lee,Hyukjoon Kwon,Yosep Kim,Hyang-Tag Lim*

TL;DR: 提出基于确定性纠缠调控的分布式变分量子计算协议，仅用局域操作与经典通信确定性调制预共享纠缠，实验验证了HeH+分子和Schwinger模型基态能量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算可扩展，但门隐形传态需反复消耗高保真Bell对、辅助比特和实时前馈，开销大、保真度低；而许多变分量子算法并不要求完全通用性，只需足够表达能力。

Result: 通过原理验证实验，估计HeH+分子和Schwinger模型的基态能量，证明可制备多种纠缠水平以匹配特定问题需求。

Abstract: Distributed quantum computing offers a scalable route to quantum information processing by entangling spatially separated processors. Although gate teleportation enables universal computation across distributed nodes, it requires repeated consumption of high-fidelity Bell pairs, ancillary qubits, and real-time feedforward, which imposes significant overhead and reduces fidelity. However, many variational quantum algorithms do not demand full universality; rather, they rely on sufficient expressibility to explore solution spaces effectively. Building on this, we propose a distributed variational quantum computing protocol based on deterministic entanglement tuning. In contrast to probabilistic filtering, our approach deterministically modulates pre-shared entanglement using only local operations and classical communication. We validate the protocol through a proof-of-principle experiment by estimating ground-state energies of the He-H$^+$ molecule and the Schwinger model, showing that diverse entanglement levels can be engineered to match problem-specific requirements. Our results suggest a practical alternative for near-term distributed quantum applications.

</details>


### [73] [Low-cost algorithm-to-execution framework for surface-code quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10965)
*Yunxin Tang,Zixuan Huo,Junxiang Huang,Zhou You,Zhirao Wang,Zhenai Ding,Yumeng Zeng,Yangyu Lu,Yiming Huang,Zongkang Zhang,Haipeng Xie,Ying Li,Jinzhao Sun,Xiao Yuan,Yuan Yao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出面向表面码量子计算的低成本"算法到执行"框架，将逻辑调度、非Clifford资源供给与容错执行组织统一链接，并在20个基准电路及应用规模任务上验证其显著降本效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅做逻辑门到编码操作的映射不足以在容错处理器上执行有用量子算法；空间组织、非Clifford资源供给与执行调度常被分别指定与优化，且物理开销需控制在实用范围内，因而需要一个可追溯、低成本的统一实现框架。

Method: 从层次化算法描述出发，构建保持依赖关系的逻辑调度，并生成可执行工作负载，刻画逻辑交互、操作并行度与时间分辨的非Clifford需求；据此联动表面码组织、资源态制备与容错执行。在直接旋转标定下比较非Clifford实现选择，并评估电路专用表面码布局对路由时延与时空体积的影响。

Result: 逻辑资源数相近的电路物理成本差异显著。QAOA振幅放大工作负载中，非Clifford实现选择相较全综合基线将时空体积降低至多241.5倍；电路专用表面码布局降低全部20个基准的路由时延估计，其中13个因通信节省超过额外空间开销而同时降低时空体积。

Conclusion: 低成本容错执行取决于计算调度与组织方式，而不能仅凭聚合的逻辑资源数量判断，统一框架有助于在实用开销约束下实现算法到执行的映射。

Abstract: The execution of useful quantum algorithms on fault-tolerant processors requires more than a mapping from logical gates to encoded operations: the spatial organization, non-Clifford resource supply, and execution schedule must also be determined while keeping physical overhead within practical limits. Although the theoretical hierarchy from logical circuits to fault-tolerant operations is well established, these implementation choices are often specified and optimized separately. Here we develop a low-cost algorithm-to-execution framework for surface-code quantum computing. From hierarchical algorithm descriptions, it constructs dependency-preserving logical schedules and an executable workload capturing logical interactions, operation parallelism, and time-resolved non-Clifford demand, thereby linking logical computation to surface-code organization, resource-state preparation, and fault-tolerant execution in a traceable workflow. We apply the framework to twenty benchmark circuits across seven algorithm families and a hierarchically composed application-scale elliptic-curve discrete-logarithm workload. Physical costs vary substantially even for circuits with similar logical resource counts. As an immediate corollary, we obtain via the Choi-Jamiolkowski isomorphism an algorithm for learning an unknown $n$-qubit Clifford unitary from $2n+\left\lceil\log_2(1/δ)\right\rceil+4$ queries, the $n$-dependence of which we show to be optimal. Abstract: Entanglement Distance (ED) was originally proposed as a geometric measure of entanglement derived from the Fubini-Study metric on the projective Hilbert space. Abstract: Quantum repeaters extend entanglement by swapping noisy elementary links. In contrast, we construct link state families of rank three and rank two that admit postselected measurement outcome branches of exponentially small probability but with strictly positive concurrence at every finite depth. Swapping experiments on a superconducting processor show that links of equal initial concurrence but different rank behave differently under postselected swapping. In the language of many-body physics, the chain is a matrix-product density operator, and full-rank bonds forbid long-range localizable entanglement, while rank-deficient bonds can sustain it.

We show that a SISIS-based QCR can operate in a regime where single-photon processes are suppressed while two-photon absorption dominates. As a result, the proposed SISIS-based QCR achieves strong suppression of dephasing-induced leakage while substantially reducing the increase in phase-flip errors associated with QCR operation. These results demonstrate that the proposed SISIS-based QCR provides an effective approach for mitigating leakage while limiting QCR-induced phase-flip errors in Kerr-cat qubits.

</details>

Method: 构建DTC储备池架构：相干Floquet演化处理局部分子图事件和表面跳跃帧，受控重置调节早期输入贡献；每个输入序列末端测量得到固定维特征向量；储备池参数在训练中保持固定，仅训练经典解码器完成抑制活性与血脑屏障渗透性分类及电子能隙预测。

Result: 在匹配输入长度与输出宽度条件下，DTC-QRC在长前缀图分类和所研究的乙烯能隙预测任务上优于回声状态网络；退相干会降低两项任务性能，表明相干传播具有重要作用；Quafu超导量子云平台实验显示，在器件噪声下成对可观测量仍保留任务信息。

Abstract: Quantum reservoir computing (QRC) uses quantum dynamics to represent input histories for prediction through a trained classical readout. Discrete time crystals (DTCs) exhibit robust subharmonic responses under periodic driving, and previous work has used their dynamics to construct DTC-QRC. Here we construct a DTC-based reservoir architecture to predict molecular properties from structural and dynamical observations. Coherent Floquet evolution processes local molecular graph events and surface-hopping frames, while controlled reset regulates the contribution of earlier inputs. Measurements at the end of each input sequence yield a feature vector of fixed dimension. Trained classical decoders use this vector for inhibitor-activity and blood--brain-barrier permeability classification and electronic-gap forecasting, while the reservoir parameters remain fixed during training. With matched input lengths and output widths, DTC-QRC outperforms echo-state networks on long-prefix graph classification and the studied ethene gap forecasting tasks. Dephasing lowers performance in both applications, consistent with a role for coherent propagation. Experiments on the Quafu superconducting quantum cloud platform show that pair observables retain task information under device noise. The architecture provides a common framework for molecular screening and time-resolved property prediction using quantum reservoir computing.

</details>


### [83] [Optimal T-Count for Block Encodings of Fermionic and Spin Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11153)
*Jiaxin Ma,Kevin J. Joven,Yuan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Clifford+T模型下确定结构化费米子与自旋哈密顿量块编码构造的非Clifford T门代价，给出ancilla压缩定理并推导多个最优下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化在允许任意干净ancilla与无限制块编码次归一化、但无中段测量与经典前馈的条件下，构造结构化哈密顿量块编码所需的T门代价，填补下界与紧致复杂度的空白。

Method: 核心技术为ancilla压缩定理：任何使用a个干净ancilla且含至多s个T门的n量子比特算符块编码，可压缩为至多min{a,n+2s}个ancilla且不增加绝对误差与T计数。结合体积覆盖论证与电路计数给出一般二次量子化哈密顿量的下界；利用稳定子零度与单量子比特态制备对Kitaev蜂窝模型给出独立下界，并配以显式LCU构造；应用于基于量子奇异值变换的模拟电路逐个编译块编码查询。

Result: 一般二次量子化哈密顿量在误差ε下最坏情况下界为Ω(n^2√(log(n^4/ε)))，与固定精度下已有上界匹配；Kitaev蜂窝模型得到Ω(n)与Ω(log(1/ε))独立下界，结合LCU构造给出紧致最坏情况缩放Θ(n+log(1/ε))；模拟T计数在相位合成与受控查询至多常数开销时，等于查询次数乘以每次查询的最优T计数。

Conclusion: 论文建立了Clifford+T下结构化哈密顿量块编码T门代价的紧致复杂度刻画，ancilla压缩定理为通用工具，所得下界对一般二次量子化哈密顿量与Kitaev模型分别为最优/紧致，并可用于分析QSVT模拟电路的T计数。

Abstract: We determine the non-Clifford $T$-gate cost of constructing block encodings of structured fermionic and spin Hamiltonians in a unitary Clifford$+T$ model, when arbitrarily many clean ancillas and unrestricted block-encoding subnormalization are allowed, but without mid-circuit measurements or classical feed-forward. Our main technical tool is an ancilla-compression theorem: any block encoding of an $n$-qubit operator with $a$ clean ancillas and at most $s$ $T$ gates can be compressed to use at most $\min\{a,n+2s\}$ ancillas, without increasing the absolute error or $T$-count. For general second-quantized Hamiltonians with bounded one- and two-body coefficients, at operator-norm block-encoding error $ε$, a volume-covering argument combined with circuit counting gives the worst-case lower bound $Ω(n^2\sqrt{\log(n^4/ε)})$, matching the existing upper bound at fixed precision. For the bond-dependent Kitaev honeycomb family on $n$ spins, we obtain independent lower bounds $Ω(n)$ from stabilizer nullity and $Ω(\log(1/ε))$ from one-qubit state preparation, established using different Hamiltonian instances. Together with an explicit LCU construction, they give the tight worst-case scaling $Θ(n+\log(1/ε))$. As an application, we evaluate the $T$-count of a Hamiltonian simulation circuit based on quantum singular value transformation, with each block-encoding query compiled separately. When phase synthesis and controlled queries add at most constant-factor overhead, the simulation $T$-count scales as the query count times the optimal $T$-count per query.

</details>


### [84] [Finite-blockade dynamics of a three-qubit ccz gate in neutral-atom arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11159)
*Paramjeet Banger,Ajay Wasan*

TL;DR: 本文对对称三中性原子体系中的有限阻塞动力学进行理论研究，构建置换对称适配哈密顿量以降低复杂度并保持精确动力学，系统分析有限Rydberg阻塞下的门操作，识别高保真、快速、低泄漏的最优工作区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解高保真多量子比特量子操作提供理论框架，并系统考察有限Rydberg阻塞（从有限到强阻塞的过渡）对门动力学的影响。

Method: 采用单一高斯激光脉冲驱动对称三中性原子体系；显式考虑向非计算Rydberg态的布居泄漏；利用体系置换对称性构建对称适配哈密顿量，在保持精确动力学的同时降低计算复杂度；系统研究Rabi频率、激光失谐与Rydberg阻塞强度之间的相互作用。

Result: 识别出兼具高保真度、快速门操作与泄漏抑制的最优工作区间；量化了主要误差机制（相位误差、布居泄漏、有限Rydberg态寿命）及其对门保真度的影响；获得实验可实现的高保真门操作。

Conclusion: 有限阻塞下的对称三原子体系可实现高保真多量子比特门操作，研究结果为多量子比特量子计算提供实用指导。

Abstract: We present a theoretical study of finite-blockade dynamics in a symmetric three-neutral-atom system, providing a framework for understanding high-fidelity multiqubit quantum operations. The transition from the finite- to the strong-blockade regime is systematically investigated to examine the effects of finite Rydberg blockade. Using a single Gaussian laser pulse, we analyze the complete gate dynamics while explicitly accounting for population leakage into non-computational Rydberg states. By exploiting the permutation symmetry of the system, we construct a symmetry-adapted Hamiltonian that reduces the computational complexity while preserving the exact dynamics. This enables a systematic investigation of the interplay among the Rabi frequency, laser detuning, and Rydberg blockade strength, leading to the identification of optimal operating regimes with high fidelity, fast gate operation, and suppressed leakage. We further quantify the dominant error mechanisms, including phase errors, population leakage, and finite Rydberg-state lifetimes, and evaluate their impact on the gate fidelity. Our results demonstrate experimentally accessible high-fidelity gate operation and provide practical guidelines for multi-qubit quantum computing.

</details>


### [85] [Generative Replay Mitigates Sample Starvation in Quantum Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11248)
*Akash Kundu,Amit Kumar Jaiswal,Sebastian Feld,Prayag Tiwari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: GenQAS 提出张量网络引导的强化学习框架，将固定矩阵乘积态热启动与优先生成式回放结合，通过学习到的局部转移模型按需合成电路转移并与真实经验混合训练 DDQN，从而缓解量子架构搜索中的样本饥饿问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习可自动完成量子架构搜索，但随着搜索空间快速扩张，有用的电路轨迹变得稀少，可扩展性受限。现有回放机制仅复用已观测转移，无法补充训练信号，存在样本饥饿问题。

Method: GenQAS 在张量网络引导下进行 RL：以固定矩阵乘积态（MPS）进行热启动，并采用优先生成式回放。学习一个局部转移模型，从真实状态-动作种子出发按需生成额外的一步合成转移，在 Double Deep Q-Network 更新中与真实经验混合使用。文中还通过随机探索分析刻画近基态电路在可及状态空间中所占区域快速收缩的现象，据此考察真实数据锚定的合成回放能否改善有效训练信号。

Result: 在 6 至 12 量子比特的化学哈密顿量基准上，GenQAS 提升了固定预算下的成功概率，并在有竞争力的能量误差下找到紧凑电路。12 量子比特时，最终成功概率较被动回放提升最高 7.0 倍；15 量子比特横场 Ising 模型上成功概率由 12% 提升至 21%；含噪 6 量子比特 BeH₂ 迁移实验中，生成式回放将达到化学精度所需步数减少 92.7%。

Conclusion: 生成式回放能够缓解量子架构搜索中的样本饥饿，支持更资源高效的电路发现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can automate quantum architecture search, but its scalability is limited when useful circuit trajectories become rare in the rapidly expanding search space. Existing replay mechanisms reuse observed transitions; the proposed learned model produces additional predicted one step transitions from real state-action seeds. Here we introduce GenQAS, a tensor network-guided RL framework that combines a fixed matrix product state warm-start with prioritized generative replay. A learned local transition model generates synthetic circuit transitions on demand and mixes them with real experience during Double Deep Q-Network updates. Under a random exploration analysis, near ground state circuits occupy a rapidly shrinking region of the accessible state space. We investigate whether real data anchored synthetic replay can improve the effective training signal in this regime. Across chemical Hamiltonian benchmarks from 6 to 12 qubits, GenQAS improves fixed-budget success probability and identifies compact circuits at competitive energy error. At 12 qubits, it improves final success probability by up to $7.0\times$ over passive replay. On a 15-qubit transverse field Ising model, GenQAS increases success probability from $12\%$ to $21\%$. In a noisy 6-qubit BeH$_2$ transfer experiment, generative replay reduces the steps to chemical accuracy by $92.7\%$. These results show that generative replay can mitigate sample starvation in quantum architecture search and support more resource efficient circuit discovery.

</details>


### [86] [Virtual quantum neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11327)
*Benchi Zhao,Xuanqiang Zhao,Yinan Li,Yingzhou Li,Giulio Chiribella*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"虚拟量子神经网络"（VQNN），通过随机采样与经典数据处理将优化空间扩展到完全正映射的线性组合，从而提升表达力与噪声鲁棒性，并在量子误差缓解、二分类和基态能量估计三类任务上验证了优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子神经网络的训练是在由酉算子（或更一般的完全正映射）参数化的量子电路族上最小化损失函数，其优化空间受限，表达力与抗噪能力有限。作者希望突破这一限制，扩大可实现的运算空间。

Method: 引入随机采样和经典数据处理，对量子神经网络的概念进行扩展，构建出"虚拟量子神经网络"（virtual quantum neural networks）。其核心是使优化空间包含完全正映射的线性组合，从而在原有酉/完全正映射参数化基础上获得更大的优化空间。

Result: 扩大的优化空间带来了更高的表达力（expressivity）和更好的噪声鲁棒性（noise robustness）。在三个代表性任务上得到验证：量子误差缓解、二分类、基态能量估计。

Conclusion: VQNN提供了一种灵活的学习范式，扩展了可实现的运算空间，并增强了近期待量子硬件的应用能力。

Abstract: Quantum neural networks are a prominent model of quantum machine learning. Their training consists in the minimization of a given loss function over a parametrized family of quantum circuits, mathematically described by unitary operators, or, more generally, completely positive linear maps. In this work, we extend the notion of quantum neural network, using random sampling and classical data processing to enlarge the optimization space in a way that includes linear combinations of completely positive maps. Our extended model, called virtual quantum neural networks, leverages its enlarged optimization space to achieve increased expressivity and improved noise robustness. These benefits are illustrated in three representative tasks: quantum error mitigation, binary classification, and estimation of ground-state energies. Overall, virtual quantum neural networks offer a flexible learning paradigm that expands the space of achievable computations and strengthens the applications of near-term quantum hardware.

</details>


### [87] [Quantum Random Access Memory Implementation Using Photon-Photon Interaction in Rydberg Atomic Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11333)
*Avirup Chakraborty,Shrabana Chakrabarti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种可扩展qRAM架构，用相位编码量子行走子取代主动开关，基于EIT-Rydberg原子系综实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qRAM是突破量子机器学习数据加载瓶颈的关键，但现有物理实现面临严重可扩展性约束：传统fanout设计需要指数级易退相干门，bucket-brigade方案需要高错误率主动开关。为克服这些局限，作者提出新架构。

Method: 将离散时间量子行走映射到腔量子电动力学框架，利用基于电磁诱导透明（EIT）的Rydberg原子系综实现。在中空波导内，强Rydberg偶极-偶极相互作用与螺线管磁场构建鲁棒路由算符，施加精确的偏振依赖相位移动，将圆偏振探测脉冲导向目标存储单元。

Result: 在强控制场区域工作可抑制出现的空间衰减，确保高度扩展存储地址的累积传输概率。该并行化架构将空间资源优化为静态门，时间复杂度达到最优对数尺度O(n log(n+m))，需要O(n+m)个物理行走子。

Conclusion: 该架构为先进量子计算实现建立了实用、容错的硬件路径，克服了传统qRAM的可扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: Quantum random access memory (qRAM) is crucial for overcoming data-loading bottlenecks in quantum machine learning; however, current physical implementations face severe scalability constraints. Traditional fanout designs demand exponential decoherence-prone gates, while bucket-brigade schemes require highly error-prone active switches. Motivated by these limitations, we propose a scalable qRAM architecture that fundamentally replaces active nodes with phase-encoded quantum walkers. Our methodology maps a discrete-time quantum walk onto a cavity quantum electrodynamics framework utilizing an electromagnetically induced transparency (EIT)-based Rydberg atomic ensemble. Inside hollow-core waveguides, strong Rydberg dipole-dipole interactions and a solenoidal magnetic field create a robust routing operator. This operator imparts precise, polarization-dependent phase shifts, steering circularly polarized probe pulses to target memory cells. Our results demonstrate that operating within a strong control field regime suppresses emergent spatial attenuation, ensuring cumulative transmission probabilities for highly scaled memory addresses. Ultimately, this parallelized architecture successfully optimizes spatial resources to static gates and temporal complexity to an optimal logarithmic scale of $\mathcal{O}(n\log(n+m))$ by requiring $\mathcal{O}(n+m)$ physical walkers, establishing a practical, fault-tolerant hardware pathway for advanced quantum computation implementations.

</details>


### [88] [Quantum-RAM Implementation Using Multiple Interacting Rydberg-Blockaded EIT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11344)
*Avirup Chakraborty,Shrabana Chakrabarti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于里德堡阻塞原子系综中量子随机行走、利用多能级电磁诱导透明（EIT）实现量子随机存取存储器（qRAM）的新型理论架构，将操作复杂度降至O(n)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有qRAM方案多依赖固态或囚禁离子系统中的几何相位门，需要低温环境且相位控制受限；本文希望利用里德堡原子间强相干偶极-偶极相互作用实现无需低温的高保真相位控制，从而构建可扩展的光子qRAM。

Method: 在腔中利用塞曼分裂构建一组平行Λ型EIT系统，将基态磁子能级对通过圆偏振激光脉冲耦合至高位里德堡态；以交叉相位调制产生条件相位移动，实现受控幺正操作。里德堡激发的EIT系统作为基本相位门，构成支持量子随机行走的二叉树节点。地址与数据量子比特编码于不同探测场并相干映射到亚稳原子态，在EIT介质内相互作用产生态选择路由所需条件相位。系统采用n+m层冷碱原子形成n能级里德堡节点二叉树，连接2^n个腔囚禁存储原子，由n+m个激光脉冲充当量子行走者与地址单元。

Result: 方案可将操作复杂度降低至O(n)，具备可扩展性，并利用集体里德堡相互作用在原子系综中实现可编程、并行的纠缠操作，形成高度相干的光子qRAM路径。

Conclusion: 该工作提出了一种无需低温、基于里德堡阻塞与多能级EIT的量子随机行走qRAM架构，通过集体里德堡相互作用实现高效条件相位门与并行纠缠操作，为可扩展光子qRAM提供了低复杂度、高相干性的实现途径。

Abstract: We propose a novel theoretical architecture for implementing a quantum random access memory (qRAM) based on quantum random walks in a Rydberg blockaded atomic ensemble utilizing multilevel Electromagnetically Induced Transparency (EIT). Unlike previous approaches that rely on geometric phase gates in solid-state or trapped ion systems, our scheme harnesses the strong, coherent dipole dipole interactions between Rydberg atoms to achieve high-fidelity phase control of photonic qubits without the need for cryogenic temperatures. By generating conditional phase shifts through cross-phase modulation in multiple lambda-type EIT systems, we realize the controlled unitary operations requisite for an efficient qRAM. In the proposed architecture, Zeeman splitting is used to engineer a set of parallel lambda systems in a cavity, where pairs of magnetic sublevels of the ground state are coupled to highly excited Rydberg states via circularly polarized laser pulses. These Rydberg excited EIT systems serve as the elementary phase gates that form the nodes of a binary tree enabling quantum random walking. Address and data qubits are encoded into distinct probe fields and coherently mapped into the metastable atomic states, where their interactions within the EIT medium generate conditional phases required for state-selective routing. The system uses $n+m$ layers of cold alkali atoms to form an $n$-level binary tree of Rydberg nodes connected to $2^n$ cavity-trapped memory atoms, operated by $n+m$ laser pulses acting as quantum walkers and address units. Our scheme offers a scalable, reducing operational complexity to $\mathcal{O}(n)$ and highly coherent pathway toward photonic qRAM, exploiting collective Rydberg interactions to realize programmable, parallel entangling operations in an atomic ensemble.

</details>


### [89] [Optimizing quantum encodings for analog simulation through dynamical algebra reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11351)
*Mariane Mangin-Brinet*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种与几何无关的框架，用于诊断并优化目标哈密顿量所生成谱代数与模拟量子器件原生控制哈密顿量所生成Krylov型算子空间之间的兼容性，并在Rydberg原子模拟处理器上编码氘核哈密顿量进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟量子计算机可直接实现连续多体动力学，但其原生控制哈密顿量只能生成受限的算子空间。因此，能否高保真复现目标哈密顿量动力学不仅取决于谱匹配，还取决于物理控制能否实际生成所需演化。作者旨在建立一种预处理判据，在器件几何与脉冲优化之前识别表示层面的不兼容性。

Method: 将编码问题表述为目标哈密顿量酉轨道上的优化问题；定义由目标哈密顿量生成的谱代数与由器件独立可调控制哈密顿量及固定初态生成的Krylov型算子空间之间的子空间重叠泛函；在U(d)上采用黎曼梯度下降最大化该光滑泛函，从而选取与原生控制代数对齐更好的谱等价表示（主角度优化）。

Result: 在Rydberg原子模拟处理器上编码氘核哈密顿量时，标准二进制编码在小系统尺寸下与该算子空间严重失配；主角度优化显著提升编码的代数兼容性；使用单一与几何无关的优化编码可大幅降低态制备保真度对原子几何的敏感性。

Conclusion: 代数可达性可作为模拟编码设计的有用预处理判据：它在器件特定几何与脉冲优化之前识别表示层面的不兼容性；代数层面可达性与态层面可达性的区分，澄清了何时需要完整编码优化、何时依赖几何的控制资源可补偿不完全的代数对齐。

Abstract: Analog quantum computers provides direct access to continuous many-body dynamics, but their native control Hamiltonians generate only a restricted operator space. Consequently, the fidelity with which they can reproduce a target Hamiltonian's dynamics depends not only on spectral agreement but on whether the physical controls can actually generate the required evolution. We introduce a geometry-independent framework for diagnosing and optimizing this compatibility between the spectral algebra generated by a target Hamiltonian $H$ and a Krylov-type operator space generated from the device's independently tunable control Hamiltonians and a fixed initial state. The encoding problem can be formulated as an optimization over the unitary orbit of the target Hamiltonian. We maximize a smooth subspace-overlap functional using Riemannian gradient descent on $U(d)$, thereby selecting a spectrally equivalent representation whose target algebra is better aligned with the native controls. We apply this framework to the deuteron Hamiltonian encoded on a Rydberg-atom analog processor. The standard binary encoding is strongly misaligned with this space for small system sizes, while principal-angle optimization substantially improves the algebraic compatibility of the encoding. Moreover, using a single geometry-independent optimized encoding substantially reduces the sensitivity of state-preparation fidelity to the atomic geometry. These results establish algebraic reachability as a useful preprocessing criterion for analog encoding design: it identifies representation-level incompatibilities before device-specific geometry and pulse optimization, while the distinction between algebra-level and state-level reachability clarifies when full encoding optimization is necessary and when geometry-dependent control resources can compensate for incomplete algebraic alignment.

</details>


### [90] [Engineering Quantum Links: Noise and Quantum-State-Degradation Metrics over Metropolitan Fiber Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11359)
*Marcello Caleffi,Laura d'Avossa,Angela Sara Cacciapuoti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在部署的7.3公里城域光纤环路上实验测量量子链路的SINR与BER，为量子网络提供工程化链路预算基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现有网络基础设施上部署量子网络需要经典通信那样的工程基础，即信道噪声及其对传输信息损伤的定量模型，而此类模型此前缺乏基于实测的建立。

Method: 在连接University of Naples Federico II两个校区的7.3公里城域光纤环（QuantumInternet.it测试床）上进行实验测量：噪声侧采用光子计数式量子SINR类比，其中暗计数为本征噪声，经典业务产生的光子（自发拉曼散射或光纤间串扰）为干扰，逐项量化各贡献；误码侧针对偏振、时间、频率三种编码自由度，分别量化信道引起的退化及其随时间漂移。

Result: 量化了暗计数、拉曼散射与串扰各自对量子SINR的贡献，并获得偏振、时间、频率三种自由度下信道致BER退化及其时漂移的实测表征。

Conclusion: 量子光纤链路与经典链路一样可由少量可测参数刻画，使部署光纤上的量子网络从物理演示转变为工程设计问题，并为量子互联网提供了量子链路预算的关键要素。

Abstract: Deploying quantum networks over existing network infrastructures requires the same engineering foundations that underpin classical communications: quantitative models of the channel's noise and of the impairments it imposes on the transmitted information. In this work, we build such a foundation on experimental measurements, grounding the quantum-network counterparts of the two cornerstone metrics of classical link characterization - namely, the SINR and the BER - on a 7.3 km deployed metropolitan-scale fiber-loop interconnecting two campuses of the University of Naples Federico II within the national QuantumInternet.it testbed. On the noise side, we adopt a photon-counting quantum analog of the SINR - in which dark counts constitute the intrinsic noise and the photons generated by classical traffic (through either spontaneous Raman scattering or inter-fiber crosstalk) constitute the interference - and we quantify each contribution directly on the deployed loop. On the bit-error side, we consider the main degrees-of-freedom available to encode a quantum state within an optical photon - namely, polarization, time, and frequency - and we quantify for each degree the channel-induced degradation and its drift over time. These results show that a quantum fiber link, like its classical counterpart, can be captured by a small set of measurable parameters, turning quantum networking over deployed fiber from a physics demonstration into an engineering design problem. Together, they provide the key ingredients of a quantum link budget for the Quantum Internet.

</details>


### [91] [Entanglement Meets Reality: A Network Engineering Assessment and Forecast of Rackable Entanglement Sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11387)
*Laura d'Avossa,Daniela Salvoni,Angela Sara Cacciapuoti,Marcello Caleffi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文从网络工程视角，对商用机架式即插即用纠缠光子源进行部署导向的性能评估，提出两类KPI并给出可蒸馏纠缠生成速率下界及量子存储相干时间需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络正从实验室走向实际部署，纠缠源作为网络的基本资源，其性能直接决定未来量子通信的可靠性、可扩展性与效率。已有研究多聚焦器件表征，缺乏面向部署的系统性性能评估。

Method: 依托大规模实验测试，在多种工作条件下评估可商用部署的机架式即插即用纠缠光子源；定义并测量两个关键性能指标：多光子产生（刻画偏离理想单对发射的程度）与纠缠质量（通过重构的双量子比特密度矩阵量化）；结合探测对速率与由密度矩阵导出的一方哈希界，估计可达到的可蒸馏纠缠生成速率下界。

Result: 给出不同工作条件下商用纠缠源的性能表现，得到兼顾对速率与纠缠质量的紧凑指标——可蒸馏纠缠生成速率下界，并将其换算为纠缠蒸馏所需的量子存储相干时间下界。

Conclusion: 该工作将光学纠缠源性能与未来量子中继节点的硬件需求直接关联，为纠缠源的部署选型与后续代际研发提供了面向网络的评价框架。

Abstract: Quantum networks are transitioning from labo- ratory experiments to real-world deployments, with entangle- ment as their fundamental resource. Since an entanglement source effectively defines a quantum network, its performance directly impacts the reliability, scalability, and efficiency of future quantum communications. In this work, we investigate rack-mountable plug-and-play entangled-photon sources from a network engineering perspective, shifting the focus from device characterization to deployment-oriented performance evaluation. Building upon an extensive experimental campaign, we assess commercially deployable hardware across multiple operating conditions, evaluate current state-of-the-art capabilities, and pro- vide an outlook on future generations of entanglement sources. We identify and evaluate two key performance indicators (KPIs): multi-photon generation, capturing deviations from ideal single- pair emission, and entanglement quality, quantified through the reconstructed two-qubit density matrix. By combining the measured detected-pair rate with the one-way hashing bound derived from each density matrix, we estimate a lower bound on the achievable distillable-entanglement generation rate, providing a compact metric that captures the trade-off between pair throughput and entanglement quality. Finally, we translate these experimental results into lower bounds on the quantum-memory coherence time required for entanglement distillation, directly linking optical source performance to the hardware requirements of future quantum repeater nodes.

</details>


### [92] [The Quantum Plumber's Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11416)
*Nicolas G. Underwood,Holger F. Hofmann,Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子反事实增益概念扩展到"量子水管工问题"：在干涉仪某条路径被阻断时，寻找最优策略以确定性识别被阻断路径，并比较多种策略在广义路径编码干涉仪及Hofmann三路径干涉仪中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作通过Kirkwood-Dirac准概率分布的负性量化了扩展Elitzur-Vaidman炸弹测试场景中的量子反事实增益，作者希望将这一量子优势识别框架推广到新的场景中。

Method: 提出"量子水管工问题"场景，针对广义路径编码干涉仪以及Hofmann三路径干涉仪（源自三能级系统五个测量上下文之间关系的研究）讨论多种策略，并通过大量模拟实验收集数据来评估不同策略的相对优劣；此外还研究了用非破坏性探测器替代阻断物的变体博弈。

Result: 通过模拟实验数据比较了各竞争策略在定位阻断物方面的相对优势，并给出了非破坏性探测器变体下的结果。

Conclusion: 量子反事实增益框架可推广至量子水管工问题，用于识别该场景下的量子优势，且不同策略的优劣可由模拟数据支持。

Abstract: Recent work quantified the notion of quantum counterfactual gain for an extended Elitzur-Vaidman bomb test style scenario, through a connection to the negativity of the Kirkwood-Dirac quasiprobability distribution. We here extend this work to identifying quantum advantage in a new scenario, which we term the ``Quantum Plumber's Problem''. In this scenario, we imagine a ``quantum plumber'', who knows that one path of an interferometer is blocked, and wants to find the optimal strategy for identifying with certainty which path this is. We discuss various strategies for a generalised path-encoded interferometer, as well as for the specific case of Hofmann's three-path interferometer, introduced in a recent analysis of the relationship between states in five measurement contexts of a three level system. We support our arguments on the relative merit of competing strategies with data collected over many simulated attempts at locating blockages. We also present results for a variant of the game in which the blockage is replaced by a non-demolition detector.

</details>


### [93] [Self-guided certification of nonlocality in quantum networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11451)
*Jean Cortés,Luciano Pereira,Aldo Delgado*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种自引导（self-guided）协议，用变分优化各方测量以最大化网络贝尔不等式违背；每步通过局域Pauli经典阴影高效评估违背度，搜索由CSPSA算法驱动。收敛后直接实现测量设置并重新评估不等式（不依赖阴影），实现"设备相关搜索"与"仅依赖统计和网络因果结构的证书"两阶段分离。在三角网络Wagon-Wheel不等式上数值验证，恢复Fritz分布的违背，并扩展至非最大纠缠与噪声态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络中的非局域性由非线性网络贝尔不等式刻画，与标准贝尔情形不同，测量优化的搜索空间更大、违背度评估计算代价高；需要一种既高效又可给出与设备无关证书的测量优化方法。

Method: 提出两阶段自引导变分协议：（1）对每一方的测量进行变分优化以最大化网络贝尔不等式违背，每步用局域Pauli经典阴影高效估计违背度，优化搜索由Complex Simultaneous Perturbation Stochastic Approximation（CSPSA）算法驱动；（2）收敛后直接实现所返回的测量设置，在不使用阴影的情况下重新评估不等式。该结构将设备相关搜索与仅依赖观测统计及网络因果结构的证书分离。

Result: 在三角网络上以Wagon-Wheel不等式进行数值模拟验证，成功恢复了Fritz分布所达到的违背值，并将该认证扩展到非最大纠缠态和含噪声态。

Conclusion: 所提自引导协议能够高效优化网络贝尔不等式测量并给出去设备相关的认证，且对非最大纠缠与噪声具有鲁棒性，为量子网络非局域性认证提供可行方案。

Abstract: Bell's theorem shows that quantum theory is incompatible with local hidden-variable models. In recent years, research on nonlocality has moved beyond Bell's original scenario to quantum networks, where multiple independent sources distribute physical systems among distant parties, giving rise to correlations certified by nonlinear rather than standard Bell inequalities. Here, we introduce a self-guided protocol that variationally optimizes each party's measurement to maximize the violation of a network Bell inequality, with the violation evaluated efficiently at each step via local Pauli classical shadows and the search driven by the Complex Simultaneous Perturbation Stochastic Approximation (CSPSA) algorithm. Once converged, the measurement settings it returns are implemented directly and the inequality is re-evaluated without shadows. This two-stage structure separates a device-dependent search from a certificate that depends only on the observed statistics and on the causal structure of the network. We validate the protocol by numerically simulating it on the triangle network using the Wagon-Wheel inequality, recovering the violation achieved by the Fritz distribution, and extending the certification to non-maximally entangled and noisy states.

</details>


### [94] [Joint Mitigation of Algorithmic and Physical Errors in Noisy Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11508)
*Shuo Zhou,Xinzhao Wang,Ruiqi Zhang,Xiaoyang Wang,Yi-Cong Zheng,Tongang Li,Shengyu Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种联合外推策略，将可调噪声强度与Trotter步长关联（λ(s)=c(sT)^{p+1}），使物理噪声与Trotter误差同阶抵消，在局域Lindblad噪声下以常数渐近开销实现噪声与Trotter误差的协同抑制，并在超导量子处理器上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 乘积公式哈密顿量模拟适合近期量子处理器，但其精度受两种相互竞争的误差限制：有限步Trotter偏差与硬件物理噪声。传统方法难以同时处理这两类误差，因此需要一种能联合抑制二者的方案。

Method: 引入联合外推策略，将每层可调噪声强度与Trotter步长绑定，取λ(s)=c(sT)^{p+1}（对应p阶乘积公式）。沿该一维路径，整段演化中的主导物理噪声修正与Trotter修正均以s^p阶进入，故可用单次Richardson外推一并抵消。基于既有的有限阶Baker–Campbell–Hausdorff截断界，推导出具有可证明的交换子标度资源保证。实验上在超导量子计算机上用学习式噪声放大演示Ising动力学，并用100比特Sparse Pauli Dynamics（SPD）模拟进行对比。

Result: 对具有局域Lindblad噪声的局域哈密顿量，该协议在所需噪声强度高于器件本征噪声底限时，能以相对无噪声Trotter外推仅常数级的渐近开销同时抑制物理噪声与Trotter误差。实验在超导量子计算机上成功演示了Ising动力学的协议；100比特SPD模拟以更少的电路设置达到了与二维Richardson外推相当的精度。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了噪声与Trotter误差协同外推的理论框架与资源保证，并给出可行的实验实现，表明近期含噪量子处理器可在可承受开销下获得高精度哈密顿量模拟。

Abstract: Product-formula Hamiltonian simulation is naturally suited to near-term quantum processors, but its accuracy is set by two competing errors: finite-step Trotter bias and physical hardware noise. We introduce a joint extrapolation strategy that ties the tunable per-layer noise strength to the Trotter step size, $λ(s)=c(sT)^{p+1}$ for a $p$-th order product formula. Along this one-dimensional path, the leading physical-noise and Trotter corrections over the full evolution both enter at order $s^p$ and can be canceled by a single Richardson extrapolation. Building on a previously established finite-order Baker--Campbell--Hausdorff truncation bound, we derive a provably commutator-scaling resource guarantee for the joint extrapolation. For local Hamiltonians with local Lindbladian noise, the protocol mitigates physical noise together with Trotter error with only a constant asymptotic overhead relative to noiseless Trotter extrapolation, provided the required noise strengths lie above the intrinsic device-noise floor. We experimentally demonstrate the protocol for Ising dynamics on a superconducting quantum computer using learned noise amplification. Complementary 100-qubit Sparse Pauli Dynamics (SPD) simulations achieve comparable accuracy to two-dimensional Richardson extrapolation with fewer circuit settings.

</details>


### [95] [One-clean-qubit spectroscopy of simulated Kitaev chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11513)
*Z. M. McIntyre,Daniel Loss*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种数字-模拟量子模拟协议，在门定义量子点阵列中通过驱动工程化的横场Ising模型提取Kitaev链的单粒子谱，利用单控制量子比特的DQC1动力学实现单一自旋测量获得全谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 门定义量子点中的自旋量子比特是可编程平台，可用于模拟凝聚态现象。Kitaev链的单粒子谱（尤其是拓扑相变特征）是重要目标，但直接获取全谱通常需要测量大量可观测量，因此需要一种高效的谱提取方案。

Method: 将Kitaev链通过Jordan-Wigner变换映射为横场Ising模型（TFIM）；在一维量子点线性阵列中以驱动工程方式实现N格点TFIM模拟；周期性根据控制量子比特状态切换模拟参数，使控制比特的动力学频闪式地对应单清洁量子比特（DQC1）计算模型的输出；从单自旋测量得到TFIM的完整谱，再由经典后处理从2^N个本征能中分离出N个单粒子能量；通过调节产生合成横场的Rabi驱动强度扫描谱特征。

Result: 控制量子比特的动力学可频闪匹配DQC1模型输出，从而由单自旋测量获得TFIM全谱；经经典后处理可提取Kitaev链的N个单粒子能量；通过改变Rabi驱动强度，可仅用单自旋测量勾勒出从平庸相到拓扑相的交叉谱特征。

Conclusion: 该协议将数字-模拟量子模拟与DQC1谱提取结合，为在量子点自旋阵列中高效表征Kitaev链的拓扑相变提供可行路径，并显著减少测量资源需求。

Abstract: Spin qubits in gate-defined quantum dots provide a highly programmable platform for simulating condensed-matter phenomena. In this work, we introduce a digital-analog quantum simulation protocol for extracting the single-particle spectrum of a Kitaev chain. The Kitaev chain is mapped onto qubits via the standard Jordan-Wigner transformation and implemented as a drive-engineered, $N$-site transverse-field Ising model (TFIM) in a linear array of quantum dots. We show that periodically toggling the analog-simulation parameters conditioned on the state of a control qubit causes the dynamics of this control qubit to stroboscopically match the output of the one-clean-qubit (DQC1) model of computation, thereby yielding the full spectrum of the TFIM from measurements of a single spin. Classical postprocessing can then be used to isolate the $N$ single-particle energies of the Kitaev chain from the $2^N$ eigenenergies of the TFIM. By varying the strength of the Rabi drive used to engineer the synthetic transverse field, the spectral signature of the crossover from the trivial to the topological regime of the Kitaev chain could then be mapped out with measurements of just one spin.

</details>


### [96] [Birth and Death of Entanglement in Hamiltonian-Driven Quantum Games under Decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11522)
*Teena Thomas,S. Balakrishnan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究基于横场Ising模型（TFIM）的双人量子博弈在振幅阻尼退相干下的表现，分析噪声对纳什均衡、收益、并发度与相干性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子博弈理论关注量子资源如何影响策略决策，而真实量子系统不可避免受到退相干影响，因此需要在物理可实现模型下考察噪声对量子博弈策略优势的作用。

Method: 采用横场Ising模型构建双人量子博弈；该哈密顿量天然产生一族纠缠门，提供物理动机明确的博弈实现；在振幅阻尼信道下，针对不同初始态与策略对，计算并分析纳什均衡、玩家收益、并发度及量子态相干性的变化。

Result: 退相干会逐步抑制量子策略优势，在最大阻尼下所有结果退化为相同的经典收益；对博弈过程中生成态的并发度与相干性分析揭示出依赖于初始态和策略的量子关联动力学，包括纠缠突然产生与突然死亡现象。

Conclusion: TFIM为实现量子博弈提供自然的纠缠门资源，但其量子策略优势对退相干敏感；量子关联动力学呈现非平凡的时间演化特征，表明物理噪声是量子博弈实际应用中的关键限制因素。

Abstract: Quantum game theory investigates the influence of quantum resources on strategic decision-making. In this work, two-player quantum games based on the Transverse Field Ising Model(TFIM) are investigated under amplitude-damping decoherence. The TFIM Hamiltonian naturally produces a family of entangling gates, enabling a physically motivated implementation of quantum games. The effects of noise on Nash equilibria, players' payoffs, concurrence and coherence of the quantum states for different initial states and strategy pairs are analyzed. The results show that decoherence progressively suppresses quantum strategic advantages, with maximum damping driving all outcomes to identical classical payoffs. The concurrence and coherence analysis of the states generated in the quantum game reveal initial state and strategy dependent quantum correlation dynamics, including entanglement sudden birth and death.

</details>


### [97] [Impossible to conjugate an unknown quantum state via a unitary evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11536)
*Dafa Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"不可共轭定理"：不存在通用物理装置或线性/幺正操作能实现对任意未知量子态的共轭操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子力学与量子信息中已有一系列"不可能定理"（不可克隆、不可删除、不可隐藏、不可翻转、不可广播等），构成量子信息限制的核心框架。作者希望将这一体系进一步推广，探寻是否存在关于量子态共轭操作的新型不可能性限制。

Method: 通过类比既有不可能定理的证明思路，针对"共轭任意未知量子态"这一任务，论证其无法由通用物理装置或线性/幺正操作实现，从而确立不可共轭定理。

Result: 证明了对任意未知量子态进行共轭操作在通用物理装置及线性/幺正操作框架下均不可能实现，得到不可共轭定理。

Conclusion: 不可共轭定理与不可克隆、不可删除、不可隐藏、不可翻转、不可广播等定理并列，丰富了量子信息理论中不可能定理的体系，揭示了量子态共轭操作的内在物理限制。

Abstract: The no-cloning theorem, the no-deleting theorem, the no-hiding theorem, the no-flip theorem, and the no-broadcasting play a crucial role in quantum mechanics and quantum information. In this paper, we propose the no-conjugating theorem which states that it is impossible to conjugate an unknown arbitrary quantum state via a universal physical device or linear/unitary operation.

</details>


### [98] [Dissipative Quantum Battery from Many-Body Scars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11595)
*Jinglin Liu,Yiming Liu,Mingdi Xu,Lei Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于耗散选择的量子多体疤痕的自主量子电池方案，利用工程化局域键耗散将系统驱动至疤痕支持的高能流形，实现接近热力学功界的高可提取功，并通过弱局域退相位获得稳定充电协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子电池需要自主、稳健的充电机制。量子多体疤痕具有非热化、相干结构保持等特性，有望作为能量存储资源，但如何利用耗散动力学实现自主充电并保持高可提取功尚待研究。

Method: 采用具有精确疤痕塔的嵌入型自旋链与广义相互作用J1--J2链，引入工程化局域键耗散将系统从被动态驱动至高能疤痕支持流形；针对理想疤痕流形中非衰减的边缘模式，进一步引入弱局域退相位以实现稳定充电，并施加显式破疤痕微扰进行对照。

Result: 充电态的单拷贝ergotropy接近熵匹配的热力学功界，同时保留疤痕阶梯相关的相干结构；弱退相位在较宽参数范围内缩短充电时间且不明显损害储存功；破疤痕微扰导致疤痕支持与可提取功的关联性损失，表明优良电池性能源于非热疤痕结构而非一般激发态的制备。

Conclusion: 耗散稳定的多体疤痕可作为自主且稳健的量子能量存储资源。

Abstract: We propose an autonomous quantum-battery protocol based on dissipatively selected quantum many-body scars. Using an embedding-type spin chain with an exact scar tower and a generalized interacting $J_1$--$J_2$ chain, we show that engineered local bond dissipation can drive the system from a passive state into a high-energy scar-supported manifold with large extractable work. The resulting charged states exhibit single-copy ergotropy close to the entropy-matched thermodynamic work bound while retaining the coherent structure associated with the scar ladder. Because the ideal scar manifold also supports nondecaying peripheral modes, we introduce weak local dephasing to obtain a stable charging protocol and find a broad regime in which the charging time is reduced without substantially degrading the stored work. Finally, an explicit scar-breaking perturbation produces a correlated loss of scar support and extractable work, demonstrating that the favorable battery performance is tied to the nonthermal scar structure rather than merely to the preparation of a generic excited state. Our results establish dissipatively stabilized many-body scars as a promising resource for autonomous and robust quantum energy storage.

</details>


### [99] [Quantum models of interaction Hamiltonian and their paradoxes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11618)
*Nicolas Gisin,Serge Massar,Jef Pauwels,Pavel Sekatski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过构造服从光锥约束、能复现两体哈密顿量的微观量子模型，定量研究微观中介体对远程系统产生的残余效应（如稳态修饰与本地控制下的退相干），并说明这些模型如何化解远距作用描述所引发的悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子物理中常直接用多体哈密顿量描述远程系统间的相互作用，但这种"超距作用"式描述若按字面理解，会带来超光速通信悖论与局域能量核算的不一致，同时忽略了中介体施加相互作用时的残余效应，如远程系统与中介体之间的纠缠，以及远程系统局域演化时产生的退相干。

Method: 研究设计上天然满足光锥约束、并可复现两体哈密顿量的简单微观量子模型；针对这些模型，定量分析微观中介体对远程系统造成的残余效应，其中包括稳态的修饰（dressing）以及在快速局域控制条件下的退相干。

Result: 对这些微观模型的定量分析揭示了中介体导致的稳态修饰与快速局域控制下的退相干等残余效应，并表明这些模型能够化解前述与超距作用描述相关的悖论。

Conclusion: 以光锥遵从的微观中介模型取代直接的超距两体哈密顿量描述，可一致地解决超光速与局域能量核算的悖论，同时显式刻画此前被忽略的残余效应。

Abstract: In quantum physics it is commonplace to model the interaction of remote systems with a many-body Hamiltonian. Taking such an action-at-a-distance description {\it à la lettre} leads to various paradoxes related to faster-than-light communication and apparent inconsistencies in local energy accounting. It also neglects residual effects, such as entanglement between the remote systems and the mediator that implements the interaction, or the decoherence that arises when the remote systems undergo local evolution. We study simple microscopic quantum models that respect the light cone by design and reproduce two-body Hamiltonians. For these models we quantitatively analyze the residual effects of the microscopic mediator on the remote systems, including dressing of stationary states, and decoherence in the presence of fast local control. We show how the models resolve the paradoxes.

</details>


### [100] [Quantum-hardware spectral co-design framework for multifrequency Rydberg electrometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11635)
*Song Huang,Guibin Tian,Fernando Quijandría,Zhengjiang Li,Zhimin He,Franco Nori,Yong Lu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将受制造约束的SPSA-Adam优化器直接耦合到全波有限元本征模仿真中，在约10^300量级的离散设计空间内实现多频Rydberg电测的量子-硬件频谱协同设计，获得同时匹配四个高Q本征模与四个Cs Rydberg跃迁的多模电测架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工程电磁硬件需满足离散的量子定义频谱约束，这一频谱协同设计问题在量子系统中具有普遍性；而传统方法在同时实现多模频谱匹配方面存在困难，因此需要一种通用且高效的协同设计框架。

Method: 将受制造约束的同时扰动随机逼近（SPSA）—Adam优化器直接耦合到全波有限元本征模仿真中；该方法无需解析梯度或伴随梯度，可在约10^300个离散构型的设计空间中搜索，最终获得同时对准四个高Q本征模与四个选定Cs Rydberg跃迁的新型多模电测架构。

Result: 优化设计对制造缺陷具有高度鲁棒性，相对频率误差低至8.42×10^-7，同时将器件长度缩减1.75×10^2倍；在相当仿真预算下，该协同设计框架的频谱匹配误差分别比协方差矩阵自适应进化策略和离散模拟退火小约10倍和41倍；预测电测的平均输入功率灵敏度提升约4.04×10^3倍。

Conclusion: 该工作表明量子-硬件频谱协同设计可作为通往多通道量子传感紧凑硬件的通用途径，克服了传统设计中多模频谱同时匹配的难题。

Abstract: Engineering electromagnetic hardware to satisfy discrete quantum-defined spectral constraints constitutes a general spectral co-design problem for quantum systems. Here we address this challenge in multifrequency Rydberg electrometry by directly coupling a fabrication-constrained simultaneous perturbation stochastic approximation (SPSA)--Adam optimizer to full-wave finite-element eigenmode simulations. Requiring neither analytical nor adjoint gradients, the method operates over a discrete design space containing approximately $10^{300}$-configurations and yields a novel multimode electrometry architecture that simultaneously aligns four high-$Q$ eigenmodes with four selected Cs Rydberg transitions. The optimized design remains highly robust to fabrication imperfections, achieving a relative frequency error as low as $8.42\times10^{-7}$ while reducing the device length by a factor of $1.75\times10^{2}$, thereby overcoming the difficulty of simultaneous multimode spectral matching encountered in conventional design. For a comparable simulation budget, the proposed co-design framework achieves frequency-matching errors approximately 10 and 41 times smaller than those of covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy and discrete simulated annealing, respectively. The electrometry is predicted to provide an average input-power-sensitivity enhancement of approximately $4.04\times10^{3}$, demonstrating quantum--hardware spectral co-design as a general route toward compact hardware for multichannel quantum sensing.

</details>
