### [101] [Certifying Adversarial Robustness of Quantum Classifiers under Known-Readout Query Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11637)
*Ji Guan,Mingyu Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究已知读出量子访问(KRQA)下量子分类器的认证对抗鲁棒性，提出仅依赖测量与有限采样结果的框架，同时给出下界（排除半径内无目标错误）与上界（见证半径内对抗态），无需层析或线路描述，并在IBM量子硬件上验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子分类器通过演化输入态并测量输出来分配标签，重复执行仅揭示标签分布。现有鲁棒性认证方法通常需要电路描述、参数、梯度或层析等内部信息，在KRQA这种仅可观测统计量的受限访问模型下难以适用，因此需要一种测量驱动的认证方法。

Method: 提出measurement-only认证框架，对每个输入返回两个互补保证：一是排除给定半径内无目标错误的下界；二是与攻击无关、见证半径内对抗态的上界。两者均可由已知读出测量与采样结果估计，具备有限样本保证。上界利用量子测量诱导的gap算子；下界将状态空间搜索松弛为在算子谱约束下对结果分布的高效优化，所得证书不弱于既有仅基于概率的证书，当谱约束起作用时可严格更强。

Result: 在多个量子分类器上评估，下界在可解实例上跟踪精确最优值，上界在标准攻击失效时仍具信息量。在IBM Quantum真实硬件上，对两个8量子比特量子神经网络各执行40次，方法即可计算两种证书，且所有测试输入均满足下界与上界的预期序关系。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在KRQA下量子分类器的鲁棒性声明可直接从可观测量统计中进行审计，无需访问内部演化、参数或梯度。

Abstract: A quantum classifier assigns labels by evolving an input quantum state and measuring the output, so repeated executions reveal only a distribution over labels. We study certified adversarial robustness for such classifiers under known-readout query access (KRQA), where an evaluator can prepare inputs, knows the quantum measurement, and observes finite-shot outcomes but cannot inspect the internal evolution, parameters, or gradients. We give a measurement-only framework that returns two complementary guarantees for each input: a lower bound ruling out untargeted errors within a radius, and an attack-independent upper bound witnessing an adversarial state within a radius. Both are estimable from the known readout measurement and sampled outcomes, require no tomography or circuit description, and admit finite-sample guarantees. The upper bound uses gap operators induced by the quantum measurement; the lower bound relaxes state-space search to an efficient optimization over outcome distributions with operator-spectrum constraints, yielding certificates that are never weaker than prior probability-only certificates and can be strictly stronger when the spectral constraints are active. Evaluations on multiple quantum classifiers show that the lower bound tracks exact optima on tractable instances, while the upper bound remains informative when standard attacks fail. We further demonstrate real-device feasibility on IBM Quantum hardware: from 40 executions of two 8-qubit quantum neural networks, our method computes both certificates, with the expected ordering between the lower and upper bounds on every tested input. Taken together, these results show that robustness claims for quantum classifiers can be audited directly from observable statistics under KRQA.

</details>


### [102] [A Near-Quartic Separation Between Certificate Complexity and Quantum Query Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11664)
*Andris Ambainis,Jānis Iraids,Martins Kokainis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 构造了总布尔函数 f 使 Q(f)=Õ(⁴√C(f))，解决了量子查询复杂度与证书复杂度关系的长期公开问题，且在对数因子内紧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子查询复杂度 Q(f) 与证书复杂度 C(f) 之间的最优关系长期未完全确定，此前缺乏能够同时达到已知下界并逼近四分之一次幂分离的显式构造，因此需要一个紧的构造来厘清二者关系。

Method: 显式构造一个总布尔函数 f，使其有界误差量子查询复杂度 Q(f) 与证书复杂度 C(f) 满足 Q(f)=Õ(⁴√C(f))；通过对函数结构（如基于特定递归/组合构造）进行复杂度分析，给出上界并证明其紧性（至对数因子）。

Result: 得到 Q(f)=Õ(⁴√C(f)) 的总布尔函数构造，该结果在对数因子范围内是最优的，从而解决了一个长期存在的公开问题。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了量子查询复杂度与证书复杂度之间近乎最优的四分之一次幂分离，推进了对 Q(f) 与 C(f) 关系上界的理解，且所给界在忽略对数因子后是紧的。

Abstract: We construct a total Boolean function $f$ for which $Q(f) = \widetilde{O}(\sqrt[4]{C(f)})$, where $Q(f)$ denotes the bounded-error quantum query complexity and $C(f)$ denotes the certificate complexity. This resolves a longstanding open question and is tight up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [103] [Lifted surgery: Fast processing with QLDPC codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11723)
*Lucas Berent,Lawrence Z. Cohen,Armanda O. Quintavalle*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出 lifted surgery 方法，实现 Abelian group algebra 码上的快速并行码手术，在保持低物理开销的同时将逻辑测量所需的综合征测量轮数减少约十倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QLDPC 码是实现低开销容错量子计算的重要候选，但逻辑操作的时间开销（尤其码手术需重复多轮综合征测量）仍是关键瓶颈，需在维持其低物理开销优势的前提下降低时间开销。

Method: 针对 Abelian group algebra 码提出 lifted surgery，保持底层码的对称性，使大实例搜索可行并利于硬件实现；利用块分解与交换代数技术刻画 lifted surgery，研究性质良好的子族并构造显式实例，如量子 radial 码 [[90,8,10]] 与 [[198,8,16]]。

Result: 所构造的 radial 码支持快速、并行且可寻址的手术，可在单轮综合征提取中测量同 Pauli 类型的任意独立逻辑算符集合；在电路级去极化噪声下基准测试表明，[[90,8,10]] 码的逻辑性能与标准码手术相当，而所需综合征测量轮数减少十倍。

Conclusion: lifted surgery 兼顾速度与并行性，为低开销容错量子计算提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes are a leading candidate for achieving low-overhead fault-tolerant quantum computing. However, the time overhead of logical operations in QLDPC codes remains a key challenge. Code surgery, a space-efficient technique for fault-tolerant logical measurements, incurs this overhead through repeated rounds of syndrome measurement. We introduce lifted surgery, a method for fast and parallel surgery on Abelian group algebra codes that maintains the low physical overhead that makes QLDPC codes attractive. Lifted surgery preserves the symmetries of the underlying code, making searches for large instances tractable and offering a natural route towards efficient hardware implementations. We utilise block decompositions and techniques from commutative algebra to characterise lifted surgery, investigate well-behaved subfamilies, and construct explicit examples. In particular, we present quantum radial codes with parameters $[[90, 8, 10]]$ and $[[198, 8, 16 ]]$ for which surgery is fast, parallel, and addressable, allowing arbitrary sets of independent logical operators of the same Pauli type to be measured in a single round of syndrome extraction. We benchmark lifted surgery under circuit-level depolarising noise and, for the $[[ 90, 8, 10 ]]$ code, find logical performance comparable to standard code surgery while requiring ten times fewer rounds of syndrome measurement. By combining speed and parallelism, lifted surgery offers a practical route towards low-overhead fault-tolerant quantum computing.

</details>


### [104] [An exchange-assisted entangling gate between 87Rb and 171Yb Rydberg atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11730)
*Han Wang,Erdong Huang,Mingrui Jing,Hongshun Yao,Xin Wang,Jinguo Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 在双物种87Rb-171Yb光镊阵列中，利用零电场下光学可达的S+S↔P+P Förster共振实现强偶极-偶极交换，通过整形光脉冲展示了0.36 μs的交换辅助受控-Z门，本征保真度99.91%，在有界扰动下仍高于99.85%，并识别出辅助性排斥范德华通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子光镊阵列支持可扩展量子信息处理；双物种87Rb-171Yb阵列结合了长寿命的镱核自旋数据比特与快速、物种选择性的铷辅助比特控制与读出。但在不引入二价原子破坏性Stark混合的前提下实现种间门，仍是未解决的难题。

Method: 识别零电场下光学可访问的S+S↔P+P Förster共振，在阵列间距下提供强偶极-偶极交换；采用整形光脉冲并在有限控制响应下实现交换辅助受控-Z门。

Result: 演示了0.36 μs的交换辅助受控-Z门，本征保真度99.91%，在有界扰动下仍高于99.85%；并识别出一条辅助性排斥范德华通道。

Conclusion: 该工作为混合量子处理器提供了完整工具箱，解决了双物种阵列中种间门易受Stark混合破坏的问题。

Abstract: Neutral-atom tweezer arrays support scalable quantum information processing. Dual-species $^{87}\mathrm{Rb}$--$^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$ arrays combine long-lived ytterbium nuclear-spin data qubits with fast, species-selective rubidium ancilla control and readout. However, realizing interspecies gates without inducing destructive Stark mixing in divalent atoms remains an outstanding problem. Here, we identify an optically accessible $S{+}S\leftrightarrow P{+}P$ Förster resonance at zero electric field, providing strong dipole-dipole exchange at array pitch. Using a shaped optical pulse under finite control response, we demonstrate a $0.36\,μ\mathrm{s}$ exchange-assisted controlled-$Z$ gate with an intrinsic fidelity of $99.91\%$, remaining above $99.85\%$ under bounded perturbations. We also identify an auxiliary repulsive van der Waals channel, providing a comprehensive toolbox for hybrid quantum processors.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum transport along a tight-binding chain connected to Markovian reservoirs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11741)
*P. S. Muraev,A. R. Kolovsky,D. N. Maksimov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了边界耦合马尔可夫库且受均匀在位退相干作用的紧束缚链中非相互作用粒子的量子输运，用主方程方法得到平均电流的精确解析解，并在无相位阻尼时给出玻色子多体解及电流分布和关联函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解退相干（在位相位阻尼）对紧束缚链量子输运性质的影响，并获得超越平均值的电流涨落信息。

Method: 采用主方程方法，在单粒子量子力学框架下求解边界耦合马尔可夫库、均匀在位退相干的紧束缚链；无相位阻尼时用反正规序特征函数给出玻色子多体完整解，计算各键电流分布函数和键间电流关联函数。

Result: 得到沿链平均电流的精确解析解，该解对非相互作用玻色子和费米子同样适用；在无相位阻尼情形，获得玻色子问题的反正规序特征函数形式的完整多体解，可计算每个键的电流分布及键间电流关联函数。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了退相干紧束缚链量子输运的精确解析处理，并扩展到多体电流统计，为分析非相互作用粒子输运的涨落与关联提供工具。

Abstract: We consider quantum transport of non-interacting particles in a tight-binding chain coupled to Markovian reservoirs at its boundaries and subject to uniform on-site dephasing. Using the masterequation approach, we derived the exact analytic solution for the mean current along the chain. This analytic solution is obtained in the framework of single-particle quantum mechanics and, thus, equally applies for transport of non-interacting bosons and fermions. In the absence of phase damping, the results are extended to the complete many-body solution for bosonic problem in the form of anti-normally ordered characteristic function. This function allows us to calculate the distribution function for the current at each bond in the chain as well as the inter-bond current correlation functions.

</details>


### [106] [Strong Converse for Quantum Capacity via a Fully Quantum Blowing-Up Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11771)
*Salman Beigi,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了任意有限维无记忆量子信道的量子通信存在指数强逆定理，并借助全量子blowing-up引理与低次多项式近似方法完成证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信道容量理论中，强逆定理刻画了传输速率超过量子容量时纠错码的性能衰减规律。此前量子情形下的指数强逆定理尚未对任意有限维无记忆信道得到完整证明，因此需要新的数学工具来建立这一基础性结论。

Method: 证明包含两个核心要素：一是全量子blowing-up引理，将低保真度编码转换为高保真度编码，其损失以信道Stinespring膨胀像上正交投影在接收方-环境二分下的投影张量范数控制；二是低次多项式构造，以指数精度逼近该投影算符的张量幂，同时控制其投影范数，从而为blowing-up论证提供所需近似。

Result: 对任意有限维无记忆量子信道，当通信速率高于量子容量时，每个编码的纠缠传输保真度随信道使用次数呈指数衰减，即建立了量子通信的指数强逆定理。

Conclusion: 该工作为量子信道容量理论提供了严格的指数强逆结论，并展示了全量子blowing-up引理与低次多项式逼近方法在量子信息论中的有效性，为后续研究提供了可推广的分析框架。

Abstract: We prove an exponential strong converse for quantum communication through every finite-dimensional memoryless channel: at any fixed rate above the quantum capacity, the entanglement-transmission fidelity of every code decays exponentially with the number of channel uses. The proof has two main ingredients. First, a fully quantum blowing-up lemma converts a low-fidelity code into a high-fidelity code, with a loss in the number of transmitted qubits controlled by the projective tensor norm of the orthogonal projection on the image of the Stinespring dilation of the channel across the receiver--environment bipartition. Second, a low-degree polynomial construction approximates the tensor power of this projector with exponential accuracy while controlling its projective norm, providing the approximation needed for the blowing-up argument.

</details>


### [107] [Absolutely Maximally Entangled States of $2q$ Parties in Every Odd Prime-Power Dimension $q$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11796)
*Mykhailo Hontarenko,Karol Życzkowski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 对任意奇素数幂 q=p^e≥3，构造了 stabilizer AME(2q,q) 态及由其单方投影得到的 AME(2q−1,q) 态，并给出闭式 bordered-circulant 矩阵生成的自对偶 MDS 码、复 Hadamard 矩阵及 AME(q+3,q) 等族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AME 态对应完美张量与最优量子纠错码，但已知存在性仅局限于有限参数范围，因此需要扩展现有参数构造。

Method: 对每个奇素数幂 q=p^e≥3，引入 F_{q^2} 上的闭式 q×q bordered-circulant 矩阵 A_q，构造 Hermitian 自对偶 MDS 码 [2q,q,q+1]_{q^2}；由此产生 stabilizer AME(2q,q) 态，并通过归一化单方投影得到 AME(2q−1,q) 态；另用选定子矩阵的显式重标度构造 AME(q+3,q) 与中间粒子数族，并给出 9 个显式母矩阵。

Result: 构造出 stabilizer AME(2q,q) 与 AME(2q−1,q) 态（q 为任意奇素数幂）；所得 MDS 码不在扩展 Reed-Solomon 类中；在适当基下振幅张量对应阶为 q^q、相位为 p 次根的归一化 q-酉复 Hadamard 矩阵；并得到 AME(q+3,q) 及中间粒子数族与 9 个显式母矩阵及单方投影。

Conclusion: 该工作在受限参数之外给出了一类新的 AME 态及关联最优码与复 Hadamard 矩阵的显式构造。

Abstract: Absolutely maximally entangled (AME) states represent an extreme form of multipartite entanglement: every reduced system containing at most half of the parties is maximally mixed. These states provide perfect tensors and optimal quantum error-correcting codes, yet their existence is known only in restricted parameter regimes. For every odd prime power $q=p^e\ge3$, we construct a stabilizer $\mathrm{AME}(2q,q)$ state whose normalized one-party projection yields a stabilizer $\mathrm{AME}(2q-1,q)$ state. A closed-form $q\times q$ bordered-circulant matrix $A_q$ over $\mathbb{F}_{q^2}$ generates a Hermitian self-dual maximum distance separable (MDS) code $[2q,q,q+1]_{q^2}$, which lies outside the extended Reed-Solomon classes. In suitable bases, the amplitude tensors define normalized $q$-unitary complex Hadamard matrices of order $q^q$ with $p$th-root phases. Additional constructions yield $\mathrm{AME}(q+3,q)$ states and families at intermediate particle numbers through explicit rescalings of selected submatrices. We also provide nine explicit parent matrices and the corresponding one-party projections.

</details>


### [108] [Oracle Separations in the Fourier Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11830)
*Atul Mantri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明了对于每个常数 k≥2，存在一个 oracle 使 FH_k⊊FH_{k+1}，从而首次给出 Fourier 层级相邻层级的 oracle 分离；同时分离了全部层级的并集与 BQP，并指出标准 oracle 与相位 oracle 在同层数下严格不等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shi 提出的 Fourier 层级用 Hadamard 层数度量量子计算能力，并猜想每增加一层都严格提升计算能力，但自提出以来对每个层级 k≥2 的相邻层级 oracle 分离问题一直悬而未决。本文旨在给出这一系列 oracle 分离，作为验证该猜想的第一步。

Method: 分离问题基于 Forrelation 构造：上一层仅需常数次查询即可求解，而第 k 层即使允许指数次查询仍然困难。下界依赖层级的一个结构性性质——Hadamard 层数限制了电路对 oracle 的查询自适应程度：使用相位 oracle 时，k 层电路可被只有 k−1 轮并行查询的算法精确模拟，从而可直接套用已有的并行查询下界；使用标准 oracle 时电路可根据先前答案分支，需另行论证。文中对相位 oracle 与标准 oracle 两种访问方式分别处理。

Result: 对每个常数 k≥2，构造出一个 oracle 使 FH_k⊊FH_{k+1}，且该结论对相位 oracle 和标准 oracle 两种访问方式都成立。此外，相对于某个 oracle，标准 oracle 在同一层数下严格强于相位 oracle，二者不可互换。还给出了全部层级并集与 BQP 相对某个 oracle 的分离。

Conclusion: 本文首次解决了 Shi 提出的相邻 Fourier 层级 oracle 分离问题，表明每增加一个 Hadamard 层确实能带来严格的计算能力提升（相对于 oracle），并揭示了不同 oracle 访问模型在层级中的不等价性，为理解 Hadamard 层数与查询自适应性的关系提供了结构性工具。

Abstract: The Fourier hierarchy $\mathrm{FH}_0\subseteq\mathrm{FH}_1\subseteq\mathrm{FH}_2\subseteq\cdots$, introduced by Shi (TCS 2005), measures a quantum computation by the number of Hadamard layers it uses. Between two layers the circuit may permute basis states and attach phases, but it may not create superposition; the layers are its only source of interference. The first level is exactly $\mathrm{BPP}$, while the second already solves Simon's problem and, through phase estimation, factors integers. Shi conjectured that every additional layer strictly increases computational power, and asked, as a first step, for oracle separations between consecutive levels. To our knowledge, the question was open at every level $k\ge2$.
  We prove that for every constant $k\ge2$ there is an oracle relative to which $\mathrm{FH}_k\subsetneq\mathrm{FH}_{k+1}$. The separating problem is built from Forrelation (Aaronson and Ambainis, STOC 2015): the level above solves it with a constant number of queries, whereas at level $k$ it stays hard even for circuits making exponentially many queries. This holds for both of the usual ways of giving a circuit access to an oracle, the phase oracle and the standard oracle, which writes its answer into a register. The two are not interchangeable: relative to an oracle, the standard oracle is strictly more powerful at the same number of layers. We also separate the union of all the levels from $\mathrm{BQP}$ relative to an oracle.
  The lower bounds rest on a structural property of the hierarchy: the number of Hadamard layers limits how adaptively a circuit can query its oracle. With a phase oracle, a circuit with $k$ layers is reproduced exactly by an algorithm making only $k-1$ rounds of parallel queries, which brings known lower bounds for such algorithms to bear. The standard oracle lets a circuit branch on earlier answers, and that case needs a separate argument.

</details>


### [109] [The cost of simulating classically tractable quantum circuits and dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11847)
*Su Yeon Chang,Supanut Thanasilp,Zoë Holmes,M. Cerezo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文质疑"多项式时间经典可模拟性"是否足以决定实践中应选择经典模拟还是量子硬件执行，通过量子采样、量子时间和经典时间三个资源指标，对若干经典可模拟电路族进行硬件级分析，发现两类方式各有优势区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判断量子演化能否被经典高效模拟是理解经典与量子计算边界的关键，但"多项式时间可模拟"只是渐近性陈述，不能直接反映实际可行性。不同多项式标度对应的计算成本差异巨大，尤其在需要昂贵预处理或量子数据采集时，因此需要从实践资源角度重新审视这一问题。

Method: 针对若干被广泛研究的经典可模拟电路族，采用量子采样复杂度、量子时间复杂度和经典时间复杂度三个资源指标，并结合代表性硬件层面的成本估算进行对比分析。

Result: 识别出即使存在多项式时间经典算法，量子模拟在部分区域仍可能更快的区间；同时也存在经典模拟更高效的区间。此外，表征未知输入态所需的大量量子采样成本，在现行云端硬件访问价格下，可能使这种多项式时间经典模拟变得过于昂贵。

Conclusion: 仅凭多项式资源的经典可模拟性保证，不足以判定应优先采用经典模拟还是量子硬件执行；实际选择需综合考量采样、时间和成本等多维资源。

Abstract: Determining whether a quantum evolution can be efficiently simulated classically is central to understanding the boundary between classical and quantum computation. However, polynomial-time simulability is an asymptotic statement, and does not by itself determine whether the (quantum-inspired) classical simulation is actually practical. Indeed, different polynomial scalings can lead to vastly different computational costs, particularly when expensive preprocessing or quantum data acquisition is required. In this work, we ask whether classically simulable quantum dynamics are in practice more resource-efficient to simulate classically than to execute directly on quantum hardware. We analyze this question using three resource metrics, quantum sample, quantum time, and classical time complexity, for several widely studied classically simulable circuit families. Using representative hardware-level estimates, we identify regimes in which quantum simulation can be faster despite the existence of a polynomial-time classical algorithm, as well as regimes in which classical simulation remains more efficient. At the same time, the large quantum sampling cost needed to characterize unknown input states can make this polynomial-time classical simulation prohibitively expensive with current cloud-based hardware access prices. Ultimately, our work indicates that guarantees of classical simulability with polynomial resources alone are insufficient to determine the preferred implementation.

</details>


### [110] [PPT states of almost maximal Schmidt number](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11849)
*Nathaniel Johnston,Benjamin Lovitz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在 $\mathbb{C}^m\otimes\mathbb{C}^n$ 上构造了一类 PPT 态，其 Schmidt 数渐近逼近较小局部维度，显著超越此前 $n/2+O(1)$ 的构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPT 态与纠缠判据密切相关，确定 PPT 态所能达到的最大 Schmidt 数（即 PPT 纠缠的下界）是量子信息理论中的核心问题；此前已知构造在同维情形仅达到 $n/2+O(1)$，与较小局部维度差距较大，亟需更优构造。

Method: 通过在 $\mathbb{C}^m\otimes\mathbb{C}^n$ 上显式构造 PPT 态，并分析其 Schmidt 数下界，得到闭式表达 $\left\lceil \frac{m+n-\sqrt{(m-n)^2+4(m+n-1)}}{2}\right\rceil$。

Result: 同维情形 $m=n$ 时 Schmidt 数下界为 $n-\lfloor\sqrt{2n-1}\rfloor$，渐近逼近 $n$，远优于先前 $n/2+O(1)$；异维情形下证明了 $\mathbb{C}^n\otimes\mathbb{C}^{3n-4}$ 上存在 Schmidt 数至少为 $n-1$ 的 PPT 态。

Conclusion: 该构造给出了目前已知渐近最优的 PPT 态 Schmidt 数下界，表明 PPT 纠缠可以接近较小局部维度，对 PPT 判据的纠缠检测能力给出了更强的限制。

Abstract: We construct PPT states on $\mathbb{C}^m \otimes\mathbb{C}^n$ that have Schmidt number asymptotically approaching the smaller local dimension. More specifically, we construct a PPT state with Schmidt number at least $$ \left\lceil \frac{m + n - ((m - n)^2 + 4(m + n - 1))^{1/2}}{2} \right\rceil. $$ In the case of equal local dimensions ($m = n$), this becomes $n - \lfloor(2n - 1)^{1/2}\rfloor$, far exceeding the previous constructions which achieved $n/2 + O(1)$. In the case of unequal local dimensions, our result shows that there exists a PPT state on $\mathbb{C}^n \otimes \mathbb{C}^{3n-4}$ with Schmidt number at least $n-1$.

</details>


### [111] [PureSuperQMA(exp) = BellPureSymQMA(poly) = QMA via Dimension-Free Bosonic Argmax](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11854)
*William Gay,Fernando Granha Jeronimo,Lenny Liu,Itai Leigh,Pei Wu,Haochen Xu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明了多个量子复杂性类之间的塌缩：QMA = PureSuperQMA = PureSuperQMA(exp) = BellPureSymQMA(poly)，推翻了此前关于包含关系严格的猜想，并由此得出精确k-局部纯态一致性问题及相关玻色/费米纯N-可表示性问题在k≥2时是QMA完全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯态一致性问题自然引出需要单一纯见证满足多个接受约束的量子证明系统，其对应类PureSuperQMA此前被定位在QMA与QMA(2)之间，Kamminga和Rudolph猜想这两个包含关系均为严格包含。本文旨在确定这些类的确切复杂性地位，澄清这一猜想。

Method: 核心技术工具是对称张量态的无维度稳定性界。模拟过程使用多项式多个见证寄存器，将随机对SWAP测试与原始验证过程的置换不变提升相结合。关键步骤是证明在对称子空间上，极值验证值与某个张量幂见证的验证值接近，且误差与维度无关。将该论证应用于两个验证模型即得到相应模拟。

Result: 证明了复杂性塌缩 QMA = PureSuperQMA = PureSuperQMA(exp) = BellPureSymQMA(poly)。其中PureSuperQMA(exp)允许指数多个检查（均匀索引且高效生成），NO情形要求逆多项式违规裕度和逆多项式比例的被违反检查数；BellPureSymQMA(poly)要求证明者提供多项式多份纯态副本。作为推论，对每个固定k≥2，精确k-局部纯态一致性是QMA完全的，相应的精确玻色和费米纯N-可表示性问题也是QMA完全的。

Conclusion: 本文否定了Kamminga和Rudolph关于PureSuperQMA的两个包含关系均严格的猜想，表明PureSuperQMA实际等于QMA。该结果统一了纯态一致性验证模型的复杂性刻画，并建立了纯N-可表示性问题的QMA完全性，为量子证明系统和量子多体物理中的一致性问题提供了新的复杂性理论基础。

Abstract: Pure-state consistency problems naturally lead to quantum proof systems in which a single pure witness must satisfy many acceptance constraints. The corresponding class $\mathsf{PureSuperQMA}$ was previously known to lie between $\mathsf{QMA}$ and $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$, and Kamminga and Rudolph (ITCS'26) conjectured that both containments are strict. In this paper, we prove the following surprising complexity collapses $$
  \mathsf{QMA} = \mathsf{PureSuperQMA} = \mathsf{PureSuperQMA}(\text{exp}) = \mathsf{BellPureSymQMA}(\text{poly}) $$ Here $\mathsf{PureSuperQMA}(\text{exp})$ allows exponentially many checks which are uniformly indexed and efficiently generated, while requiring an inverse-polynomial violation margin and an inverse-polynomial fraction of violated checks for the NO cases. $\mathsf{BellPureSymQMA}(\text{poly})$ is a related model that requires the prover to give the verifier polynomially many copies of a pure state, which the verifier measures separately with logarithmic output length for each local measurement, before processing the outcomes jointly.
  The main technical ingredient is a dimension-free stability bound for symmetric tensor states. Our simulations use polynomially many witness registers and combine a random-pair SWAP test with a permutation-invariant lift of the original verification procedure. The key step is to show that, on the symmetric subspace, the extremal verification value is close to that of some tensor-power witness with dimension-independent error. Applying this argument to the two verification models yields both simulations. As a consequence, exact $k$-local pure-state consistency is $\mathsf{QMA}$-complete for every fixed $k\ge2$, and so are the corresponding exact bosonic and fermionic pure $N$-representability problems.

</details>


### [112] [Taking Advantage of Noise in Distributed Random Quantum Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11898)
*J. Montes,F. Borondo,Gabriel G. Carlo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文用约化的二阶矩转移矩阵理论分析分布式随机电路中的Pauli二阶矩，将噪声谱分解为径向（耗散损失）与角向（Haar混合）两支，给出弱噪声判据：仅当噪声对纵向Bloch分量抑制强于横向时才利于角向随机化。三种信道中只有振幅阻尼局部有利，去极化中性，退相位被径向损失主导；多核架构下径向泄漏服从普适一阶律，角向分支需数值追踪。结果显示存在狭窄的真实噪声辅助Haar混合窗口，但不支持噪声带来普适加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 噪声会让随机量子电路"看起来更快随机化"，但实际上可能只是耗散而非真正的幺正动力学随机化。在模块化处理器中，局域门只随机化各核心，稀缺的核间通信需将随机性扩散到整个器件，因此必须区分"有用噪声随机化"与"单纯耗散"。

Method: 构建约化的二阶矩转移矩阵理论，描述受振幅阻尼、去极化和退相位噪声影响的分布式随机电路中Pauli二阶矩的演化。核心步骤是将含噪谱分解为径向分支（描述非单位Pauli权重的耗散损失）和角向分支（描述存活非平凡子空间内的Haar型混合）。针对多核架构，推导径向泄漏的普适一阶律，并对角向分支在不同信道、拓扑和核心划分下进行数值追踪。

Result: 弱噪声判据表明，仅当噪声对纵向Bloch分量的抑制强于对横向平面的抑制时，噪声才有助于角向随机化。据此，三种信道中振幅阻尼是唯一局部有利情形，去极化噪声中性，退相位被径向损失主导。多核架构中得到径向泄漏的普适一阶律；数值结果揭示了狭窄的真实噪声辅助Haar混合窗口，在振幅阻尼下最为明显，但排除了噪声带来普适加速的可能。

Conclusion: 该框架成功区分了有用的噪声随机化与单纯耗散，说明噪声不会带来普适的随机化加速，但在特定条件（尤其是振幅阻尼）下存在有限的噪声辅助Haar混合窗口。

Abstract: Adding noise can make a random quantum circuit look faster without making its unitary dynamics more random. This distinction is especially relevant in modular processors, where local gates randomize each core and scarce inter-core communication must spread that randomness across the full device. In this paper, we study this problem with a reduced second-moment transfer-matrix theory for Pauli second moments in distributed random circuits affected by the amplitude-damping, depolarizing, and dephasing noise channels. The key step is to resolve the noisy spectrum into two branches: a radial branch, describing dissipative loss of non-identity Pauli weight, and an angular branch, describing Haar-like mixing within the surviving nontrivial sector. This separation gives a simple weak-noise criterion: noise is useful for angular randomization only when it suppresses the longitudinal Bloch component more strongly than the transverse plane. Among the three channels considered, this selects amplitude damping as the only locally favorable case, while depolarizing noise is neutral and dephasing is dominated by radial loss. For multicore architectures, we derive a universal first-order law for radial leakage and track the angular branch numerically across different channels, topologies, and core partitions. The results reveal narrow windows of genuine noise-assisted Haar mixing, most clearly for amplitude damping, but rule out a generic speed-up by noise. The framework therefore distinguishes useful noisy randomization from mere dissipation.

</details>


### [113] [EFI Pairs Without One-Way Puzzles: Oracle Separations from Communication Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11901)
*Atul Mantri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个经典随机谕言机，在该谕言机下单向谜题（one-way puzzles）不存在（即使验证方无界），而 EFI 对（EFI pairs）仍能抵抗所有"全程经典查询且持有该谕言机建议、仅在最后做一次叠加查询"的区分者。证明将该安全归约到通信复杂度（Vector-in-Subspace），并用随机矩阵理论处理最后的叠加查询。同一攻击还给出无预共享纠缠的经典消息协议中任意量子方的经典模拟，因而在该谕言机下也不存在量子性证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EFI 对与单向谜题是量子密码学最小假设的两个主要候选：单向谜题蕴含 EFI 对，但反向是否成立尚未解决。本文旨在给出一个分离二者的相对化框架，从而在谕言机模型下探讨两者的不等价性，并进一步刻画量子多项式时间在经典输入输出任务上是否有优势。

Method: 构造单一经典谕言机：该谕言机回答关于量子采样器输出概率的一切问题（从而消除谜题），并隐藏一个 Haar 随机的半维子空间。安全性证明分两部分：(1) 将经典查询情形归约到通信复杂度，把敌手模拟进二人协议，使其不优于 Vector-in-Subspace（Klartag–Regev, STOC 2011）的最佳经典协议，无论谕言机如何计算；(2) 用随机矩阵理论工具约束最后那一次叠加查询。

Result: 在该谕言机下：单向谜题不存在（即使验证方无界）；EFI 对能抵抗拥有谕言机经典建议、仅最后做一次叠加查询的任意区分者；同一攻击给出无预共享纠缠的经典消息协议中任意量子方的经典模拟，故相对该谕言机不存在量子性证明。量子多项式时间在任何经典输入输出任务上均无优势，而两量子态仍不可区分。

Conclusion: 本文给出了 EFI 对与单向谜题在相对化意义下的分离，并表明在经典输入输出任务上量子多项式时间相对该谕言机无计算优势。作者进一步提出若干猜想，讨论如何去除对叠加查询次数的限制。

Abstract: EFI pairs (Brakerski, Canetti, and Qian, ITCS 2023) and one-way puzzles (Khurana and Tomer, STOC 2024) are the leading candidates for the minimal assumption of quantum cryptography. The first are efficiently preparable quantum states, statistically far yet computationally indistinguishable; the second are classical puzzles, easy to sample and hard to solve. One-way puzzles imply EFI pairs, and whether the converse holds is open.
  We construct a single classical oracle relative to which one-way puzzles do not exist, even with an unbounded verifier, while an EFI pair survives every distinguisher that queries the oracle classically throughout and holds advice about it, making its one superposition query at the end. The oracle answers every question about the output probabilities of quantum samplers, which removes the puzzles, and hides a Haar-random half-dimensional subspace.
  To prove security we reduce it to communication complexity. An adversary whose knowledge of the subspace arrives as classical query answers can be simulated inside a two-party protocol against the party holding it, so it does no better than the best classical protocol for Vector-in-Subspace (Klartag and Regev, STOC 2011), whatever the oracle computes. That argument does not cover the superposition query, which we bound instead using tools from random matrix theory.
  The same attack gives a classical simulation of any quantum party in a classical-message protocol with no entanglement shared in advance, so relative to the oracle there is no proof of quantumness either. Quantum polynomial time therefore offers no advantage on any task with classical inputs and outputs, while the two quantum states stay indistinguishable. We state conjectures on removing the restriction on superposition queries.

</details>


### [114] [A Chip-scale Space-time Multiplexed Gaussian Boson Sampling Processor Beyond 10,000 Photons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11922)
*Yu-Xuan Fu,He-Yu Shen,Ke-Ming Hu,Jun-Jie He,Yun-Long Nie,Hang Song,Bao-Jing Liu,Le-Si Yang,Xiao-Yu Wu,Pei-Lin Du,Yu-Ze Zhu,Yi Xie,De-Hui Huang,Yu-Fei Liu,Hai Yan,Jin-Hong Chen,Yu-Lin Yu,Chuan-Yan Peng,Wen-Hao Zhou,Feng Lu,Yu-Quan Peng,Chen-Shuo Xia,Zhi-Chao Wang,Zhe-Han Li,Lin Chen,Chao-Yang Zhang,Chun-Yan Jin,Yan-Rong Zong,Xiao-Yun Xu,Jian-Peng Dou,Xiao-Tian Fang,Pei-Qi Zhou,Hao Tang,Chang-Shun Wang,Lin Yang,Xian-Min Jin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道首个芯片级时空复用高斯玻色采样（GBS）系统，基于薄膜铌酸锂单片集成高速电光调制器、片上延迟线与时间-空间复用干涉网络，在4 GHz时钟下1毫秒内探测到最多11,059个光子，并进一步将硬件重构为GBS驱动的世界模型，预测误差低于经典回声状态网络基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GBS系统面临光学对准严格、相位不稳定、可编程性有限等实际瓶颈，难以实现可扩展的工程部署；芯片级时空复用架构有望解决上述限制，但要求晶圆级芯片同时满足低损耗、高精度与高速调制等严苛条件。

Method: 在薄膜铌酸锂芯片上单片集成高速电光调制器、片上延迟线以及时间-空间复用干涉网络，构建芯片级时空复用GBS系统，工作于4 GHz时钟频率。

Result: 在1毫秒内实现最多11,059个光子探测事件；并将光子硬件重配置为GBS驱动的世界模型用于物理动力学建模，相比经典回声状态网络（ESN）基线，在更少可训练读出参数下取得更低的预测误差。

Conclusion: 结果验证了可扩展光子量子硬件方案的可行性，为未来GBS量子系统的多功能可编程应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Gaussian boson sampling (GBS) has emerged as a leading photonic paradigmfor demonstrating quantum computational advantage. Nevertheless, state-ofthe-art GBS setups face practical barriers including stringent optical alignment, phase instability, and limited programmability, which impede scalable engineering deployment. The chip-scale space-time multiplexed architecturepromises to resolve these constraints, yet it strongly demands wafer-scale chipcapabilities to simultaneously satisfy stringent requirements on low loss, highprecision and high-speed modulation. Here we report the first chip-scale spacetime multiplexed GBS system, monolithically integrating high-speed electrooptic modulators, on-chip delay lines, and a time-space multiplexed interferometric network on a thin-film lithium niobate chip, operating at a 4-GHz clockrate with detection events of up to 11,059 photons within 1 millisecond. Beyond benchmarking quantum advantage, we further reconfigure the photonichardware into a GBS-powered world model for modelling physical dynamics,which achieves lower prediction error with fewer trainable readout parameters compared with a classical echo state network (ESN) baseline. Our resultsvalidate the feasibility of our endeavor towards scalable photonic quantumhardware, and pave the way for the versatile programmable applications offuture GBS quantum systems.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Nonthermal Solar Stalling of an Inelastic Scalar Signal in Xenon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10636)
*XinXin Qi,Hao Sun*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 构建无反常 U(1)_{B-3L_τ} 模型以解释 LZ 实验在 248 keV 处的核反冲候选事例，并评估其在太阳中微子与加速器限制下的生存性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN 扩展能量搜索报告了一个 248 keV 核反冲候选事例，需要理论解释；同时检验太阳能中微子对电弱非弹性暗物质的限制是否可以模型无关地推广到轻媒介子标量实现。

Method: 构造无反常 U(1)_{B-3L_τ} 模型：840 GeV 复标量发生分裂为 320 keV 的吸热转变，160 MeV 矢量媒介子携带暗电荷 Q_χ=1；计算 LZ 公共响应重构中的接受事例数、标量湮灭遗迹丰度、物理核子比 f_n/f_p 对太阳铁捕获的抑制，并演化被捕获粒子在能量、角动量和内态上的分布，包含有限碰撞率、热核速度和激发态衰变。

Result: 接受事例数为 0.958；标量湮灭给出 Ωh^2=0.1200；f_n/f_p=-0.86985 使太阳铁捕获受抑，C_⊙=1.09×10^19 s^-1；分布停滞于 r_eff=0.194–0.198 R_sun，对应 Γ_ann=(1.33–1.38)×10^15 s^-1，比模型相关的 IceCube 公共数据代理低三个数量级以上，且低于专门的 NA64 B-L 限制。

Conclusion: 太阳能中微子对电弱非弹性暗物质的限制不能模型无关地迁移到轻媒介子标量实现。

Abstract: The LUX-ZEPLIN experiment has reported one nuclear-recoil candidate at $248\kev$ in an extended-energy search. We construct an anomaly-free $U(1)_{B-3L_τ}$ model in which an $840\gev$ complex scalar undergoes an endothermic transition with splitting $320\kev$. A $160\mev$ vector with dark charge $Q_χ=1$ gives $0.958$ accepted events in a public LZ-response reconstruction while scalar annihilation yields $Ωh^2=0.1200$. The physical nucleon ratio $f_n/f_p=-0.86985$ suppresses solar iron capture, leaving $C_\odot=1.09\times10^{19}\,{\rm s}^{-1}$. We then evolve captured particles in energy, angular momentum, and internal state, including finite collision rates, thermal nuclear velocities, and the excited-state decay. The distribution stalls at an equivalent radius $r_{\rm eff}=0.194$--$0.198\rsun$, for which $Γ_{\rm ann}=(1.33$--$1.38)\times10^{15}\,{\rm s}^{-1}$, below a model-specific IceCube public-data proxy by more than three orders of magnitude. The benchmark also lies below the dedicated NA64 $B-L$ limit. Thus solar-neutrino bounds on electroweak inelastic dark matter do not transfer model-independently to light-mediator scalar realizations.

</details>


### [116] [Implications of Inelastic Dark Matter for Primordial Dark-Star Evolution: Kinematic Thresholds and Nonthermal Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10662)
*Qinxun Li,Shengyi Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非弹性（吸热）暗物质在原始暗星中的俘获过程，揭示其相对于弹性俘获的两个新特征：存在紧密度阈值且俘获率在阈值以上呈二次方开启；非热束缚轨道粒子可形成持久储存库，且储存库会急剧压缩后停滞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LUX-ZEPLIN近期报告了248-keV核反冲候选事件，重新激发了学界对吸热非弹性暗物质的兴趣，因此有必要考察其在原始暗星中的俘获行为，以理解暗星演化中暗物质的动力学角色。

Method: 通过分析吸热非弹性俘获的运动学条件，引入由星体质量、暗物质-原子核约化质量和质量劈裂决定的运动学半径R_kin∝μ_χ M_⋆/δ作为紧密度阈值判据；追踪完整的态变碰撞链，考察新俘获粒子从非热束缚轨道到储存库的演化路径。

Result: 俘获仅在星体生长越过紧密度阈值后开启，且俘获率在阈值以上呈二次方增长；吸热运动学使通常瞬态的非热束缚群体保持空间延展，形成持久储存库；储存库在态变碰撞链中急剧压缩后停滞，原因是处于紧密度相关轨道能以下的基态粒子在星内无允许的上散射通道；约百分之一的半径收缩即可重新开启弛豫过程。

Conclusion: 上述运动学结果不依赖于激发态是即时衰变还是长寿命；因此非弹性俘获无法补充热湮灭核心，俘获群体形成演化的轨道分布，为恒星与暗物质在核心和储存库之间的协同演化奠定基础，相关研究将在姊妹篇中展开。

Abstract: The recent 248-keV nuclear recoil candidate reported by LUX-ZEPLIN has renewed interest in endothermic inelastic dark matter, motivating us to examine its capture in primordial dark stars. Relative to the conventional elastic-capture picture in dark star evolution, endothermic capture adds two qualitative features. First, it opens only after the growing star crosses a compactness threshold, expressed as a kinematic radius $R_{\rm kin}\proptoμ_χM_\star/δ$ set by the stellar mass, the dark-matter--nucleus reduced mass and the mass splitting. The accreting growth carries the star through the threshold, and the capture rate turns on quadratically above it. Second, although newly captured particles generically begin on nonthermal bound orbits, endothermic kinematics can keep this normally transient population spatially extended, turning it into a persistent reservoir rather than an intermediate step toward a thermal core. Following complete chains of state-changing collisions, we find that the reservoir compacts sharply and then stalls, because a ground state particle below a compactness-dependent orbital energy has no allowed up-scatter anywhere in the star. A percent-level radius contraction reopens the relaxation. These kinematic results do not depend on whether the excited state decays promptly or is long-lived. As a result, inelastic capture does not replenish a thermal annihilation core. The captured population forms an evolving orbital distribution that sets up the co-evolutionary dynamics between the star and the dark matter in the core and the reservoir, which we develop in a companion paper.

</details>


### [117] [The Dark Dimension and Majorana Neutrinos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10668)
*Arturo de Giorgi,Dhruv Pasari*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对Swampland中"介观暗维度"（0.1–10 μm、含keV量级体Majorana费米子）情形，推导了体Majorana中微子的地面实验约束随紧致化半径的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Swampland纲领近期进展暗示可能存在尺寸为0.1–10 μm的介观暗维度，其中包含1–10 keV的体Majorana费米子；作者希望据此给出对体Majorana中微子的地面实验约束。

Method: 先确定质量谱与混合结构，然后用Daya Bay中微子振荡数据、KATRIN β衰变上限与KamLAND-Zen无中微子双β衰变（0νββ）极限进行检验；对0νββ给出有效Majorana质量的闭式表达式；并分析层级式Majorana质量的影响。

Result: 当核动量超过紧致化尺度与Majorana尺度时，Kaluza–Klein塔会有效屏蔽0νββ。在暗维度窗口内，β衰变给出最强约束，将允许的Yukawa耦合压低至O(10⁻³)；振荡与0νββ探测分别对更小和更大的Majorana质量更敏感，具有互补性。在暗维度假设之外，层级式Majorana质量可弱化约束。

Conclusion: 在暗维度框架下，β衰变是限制Yukawa耦合的主导实验，振荡与0νββ作为互补探针覆盖不同Majorana质量区间。

Abstract: Recent developments in the Swampland program motivate the existence of a mesoscopic Dark Dimension of size $0.1-10~μ$m with bulk Majorana fermions in the $1-10$ keV range. Motivated by this, we derive terrestrial constraints on Majorana bulk neutrinos as a function of the compactification radius. After determining the mass spectrum and mixing structure, we confront the model with Daya Bay neutrino-oscillation data, the KATRIN beta-decay bound, and the KamLAND-Zen neutrinoless double beta decay limit. For the latter, we obtain a closed-form expression for the effective Majorana mass and show that the Kaluza-Klein tower efficiently screens neutrinoless double beta decay when the nuclear momentum exceeds the compactification and Majorana scales. In the Dark-Dimension window, beta decay provides the strongest constraint, pushing the allowed Yukawa couplings down to $\mathcal{O}(10^{-3})$. Oscillation and neutrinoless double beta decay searches remain complementary, probing smaller and larger Majorana masses, respectively. For completeness, outside the Dark-Dimension assumptions, we investigate how hierarchical Majorana masses can weaken the constraints.

</details>


### [118] [Ultralight Axial Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10671)
*Anson Hook,Junwu Huang,Kevin Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 超轻暗物质可能是轴矢量场，其与费米子的轴向矢流或暗电偶极矩耦合可产生比轴子暗物质更强的自旋力矩；其"轴子-光子"类比耦合需光子质量，导致背景磁场效应受压，最强探测来自偏振测量、超导腔电场和宇宙微波背景；大质量轴矢量与二形式对偶，故结论亦适用于弦论动机的Kalb-Ramond暗物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超轻暗物质除轴子外还可能为轴矢量场，这一简单可能性激发了新实验的需求，且其耦合机制可能带来更强的可探测信号。

Method: 理论分析轴矢量场与费米子的轴向矢流及暗电偶极矩算符耦合，并研究其"轴向-光子"耦合（类比轴子-光子耦合，但需光子质量）；结合对偶性将结果推广至大质量二形式（Kalb-Ramond）暗物质。

Result: 轴矢量耦合相对于轴子暗物质产生增强的自旋力矩；在"轴向-光子"耦合下背景磁场效应被抑制，最强实验探针为偏振测量、超导腔中的电场以及宇宙微波背景。

Conclusion: 超轻轴矢量暗物质在自旋力矩与光子耦合方面具有区别于轴子的可探测特征，且通过对偶性其结论可扩展至弦论中的Kalb-Ramond暗物质。

Abstract: Ultralight dark matter could be an axial vector field, a simple possibility which motivates new experiments. Couplings to fermions, through an axial vector current or a dark electric dipole moment operator, lead to enhanced spin torques compared to axion dark matter. An axial vector also has an analogue of the axion-photon coupling, though its consistent realization requires a photon mass. Under this "axial-photon" coupling, the effect of a background magnetic field is suppressed, and the strongest experimental probes involve polarimetry, electric fields in superconducting cavities, and the cosmic microwave background. Since a massive axial vector is dual to a massive two-form, our results also apply to "Kalb-Ramond" dark matter motivated by string theory.

</details>


### [119] [On the Numerical Integration of One-Loop Cosmological Collider Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10673)
*Michael Borinsky,Aidan Herderschee,Qianshu Lu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出"约化Schwinger方法"数值计算暴胀双谱的单圈in-in图，首次实现完整单圈双谱在全运动学范围内的直接数值评估，并构建模板与CMB数据比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一大类暴胀模型中，主导的非高斯双谱来自单圈而非树图过程；但现实相关的单圈贡献除最简单的泡泡图外基本未被探索。对一般质量和耦合，三角图贡献未必相对泡泡图被压低，因此现有研究不足以满足观测需求。

Method: 1) 用Witten-Feynman参数化将de Sitter圈积分化为由Symanzik图多项式支配的广义Euler-Mellin积分；2) 发现重质量时被积函数多项式指数变为复数，标准数值技术出现符号问题；3) 引入约化Schwinger方法，解析计算高度振荡的子积分得到Gauss超几何核，其余积分用标准数值求积处理。

Result: 通过复现以₃F₂函数表示的树级双谱已知解析结果验证算法；随后应用于一般运动学下的单圈泡泡图和三角图贡献，得到首个全运动学范围有效的完整单圈双谱直接数值评估结果。

Conclusion: 该数值方法克服了重质量下的符号问题，实现了完整单圈双谱的精确数值计算；基于此构建双谱模板并与CMB数据进行对比，为观测检验提供支持。

Abstract: In a broad class of inflationary models, the leading non-Gaussian bispectrum arises from one-loop rather than tree-level processes. However, realistic one-loop contributions remain largely unexplored for phenomenologically relevant masses beyond the simplest bubble diagrams. This is insufficient for observational purposes because, for generic masses and couplings, triangle contributions need not be suppressed relative to their bubble counterparts. We present a numerical method for evaluating scalar one-loop in-in diagrams contributing to the inflationary bispectrum at fully general external momenta and masses. First, the Witten-Feynman parameterization reduces de Sitter loop integrals to generalized Euler-Mellin integrals governed by Symanzik graph polynomials. Unfortunately, the exponents of the polynomials in the integrand become complex for sufficiently heavy masses, leading to sign problems when evaluating the integral using standard numerical techniques. To remedy this problem, we introduce the reduced Schwinger method, which evaluates a highly oscillatory subintegral analytically to obtain a Gauss hypergeometric kernel, leaving the remaining integrals to standard numerical quadrature. We validate the algorithm by reproducing known analytic results for the tree-level bispectrum in terms of ${}_3F_2$ functions, and then apply it to the one-loop bubble and triangle contributions at general kinematics. This yields the first direct numerical evaluation of the complete one-loop bispectrum, valid across the full kinematic range. Using these numerical results, we construct bispectrum templates and compare them with CMB data.

</details>


### [120] [Re-examining the sensitivity of JWST to decaying axion dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10674)
*Caleb Gemmell,Christopher Dessert,Andrea Caputo,Joshua W. Foster*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过定制的数据约简与非参数推断方法，解决了JWST搜寻eV尺度QCD轴子暗物质时各分析间灵敏度相差一个数量级的分歧，给出稳健的衰变限制，并排除500 meV至2.5 eV质量范围的QCD轴子暗物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: eV尺度QCD轴子若构成观测到的暗物质丰度，应在红外波段产生光子谱线，可为JWST观测。但现有针对JWST灵敏度的预测与分析结果不一致，灵敏度分歧高达一个数量级，缺乏共识，且与先前预测的一致性不明，因此需厘清这些矛盾。

Method: 采用定制（bespoke）的数据约简流程与灵活的非参数推断方法，对公开数据集进行分析，得到受控且稳健的轴子暗物质衰变限制，并与先前预测灵敏度对比。

Result: 所得限制与先前预测的灵敏度一致；证明此前声称的最强灵敏度超过了从相应数据集出发任何分析所能达到的水平。利用NIRSpec数据排除了500 meV至2.5 eV质量的QCD轴子暗物质，并在100 meV至500 meV质量区间对ALP暗物质给出与其他天体物理限制互补的约束。

Conclusion: 灵敏度存在系统性限制，除非仪器建模与数据约简流程显著改进，否则不太可能通过持续的数据采集或重新分析获得提升。

Abstract: An eV-scale QCD axion comprising the observed dark matter (DM) abundance is expected to generate a photon line at infrared energies that would be observable or near-observable in data collected by the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST), as might more general axion-like particles (ALPs) over a broader range of masses and couplings. This has motivated a number of efforts to either forecast JWST sensitivities to a QCD axion or realize them through analyses of publicly available datasets. At present, no consensus exists; leading analyses disagree by as much as an order of magnitude in terms of axion-coupling sensitivity, implying an orders-of-magnitude discrepancy in raw flux density sensitivity, and consistency between the analyses and prior forecasts is unclear. We address these outstanding discrepancies with a bespoke data reduction and flexible nonparametric inference procedure that lead to well-controlled and robust limits on the decay of eV-scale axion DM, consistent with previously forecasted sensitivities. We further demonstrate that the strongest previously claimed sensitivities exceed those attainable by any analysis of the datasets from which they were derived. We exclude QCD axion DM for masses between $500\,\mathrm{meV}$ and $2.5\,\mathrm{eV}$ using NIRSpec data, while setting limits on ALP DM complementary to other astrophysical constraints at masses between $100\,\mathrm{meV}$ and $500\,\mathrm{meV}$. However, we find the sensitivities to be systematically limited, and therefore unlikely to be improved upon by ongoing data collection or re-analysis unless instrumental modeling and data reduction pipelines improve considerably.

</details>


### [121] [Computationally Efficient Description of Medium Response to Jets in Heavy Ion Collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10679)
*Jorge Casalderrey-Solana,José Guilherme Milhano,Daniel Pablos,Krishna Rajagopal,Xiaojun Yao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出高效尾迹（Efficient Wake）方法，利用线性化流体力学模板解加旋转与助推，快速计算重离子碰撞中喷注尾迹产生的强子分布，结果与全(3+1)维非线性流体力学吻合，且比后者快数万倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重离子碰撞中高能部分子穿过夸克胶子等离子体时沉积能量与动量，激发流体尾迹并影响强子分布；原有Hybrid Model中采用的过度简化方法对尾迹强子分布描述不佳，而全非线性流体力学计算代价过高，因此需要兼顾精度与效率的新方法。

Method: 基于线性化流体力学的线性叠加性，在Bjorken流背景（无横向流）中预先计算模板解；通过适当的旋转与助推，将模板中的涨落映射到冻结面上某点的涨落，从而纳入纵向膨胀与横向径向流对尾迹演化和粒子产生的影响。

Result: 与全(3+1)维非线性流体力学结果对比，二者吻合合理；相比Hybrid Model中旧的简化方法，新方法对喷注尾迹来源强子分布的描述显著更好；计算效率极高，至少比全非线性流体力学快数万倍。

Conclusion: 该高效尾迹方法在保证描述精度的同时大幅降低计算成本，预期将其应用于重离子碰撞喷注的蒙特卡洛分析（如Hybrid Model）后，可显著改善诸多喷注及喷注子结构观测量中软成分与实验数据的符合程度。

Abstract: We develop an Efficient Wake procedure for computing the distribution of hadrons originating from jet wakes in heavy ion collisions - the hydrodynamic response of a droplet of quark-gluon plasma to the energy and momentum deposited in it by high-energy partons propagating through it. The procedure employs the linearity of linearized hydrodynamics and takes account of the effects of both longitudinal expansion and transverse radial flow on the hydrodynamic evolution of the wakes and on the resulting particle production at the freezeout hypersurface. It makes repeated use of template solutions to linearized hydrodynamics in a Bjorken flow background with no transverse flow, templates that need only be computed once, and uses suitable rotations and boosts to map fluctuations from these templates to fluctuations at a point on the freezeout hypersurface in a way that incorporates the effects of the radial flow. We benchmark this procedure by comparing its results to results obtained from full $(3+1)$-dimensional nonlinear hydrodynamics calculations, find reasonable agreement, and find that our Efficient Wake procedure yields a much better description of the distribution of hadrons originating from jet wakes than does the older oversimplified procedure employed in the Hybrid Model. And, the Efficient Wake procedure is computationally efficient: it is at least tens of thousands of times faster than full nonlinear hydrodynamics calculations. Hence, we anticipate that when our new procedure is implemented in Monte Carlo analyses of jets in heavy ion collisions, for example in the Hybrid Model, it will greatly improve the description of the soft component of many jet and jet substructure observables as compared to experimental data.

</details>


### [122] [Opening the Topological Portal to Dark Sectors with Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10681)
*Joe Davighi,Admir Greljo,Lia Schöneweiß,Nudzeim Selimovic*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 构建了一个紫外完备模型，通过矢量媒介子（规范轻子厌恶性重子流 $B_1+B_2-2B_3$）连接QCD与赝Nambu-Goldstone暗物质，计算超出有效场论适用范围的热共湮灭并检验对撞机与低能约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究QCD与赝Nambu-Goldstone暗物质之间"拓扑门户"的现象学，并解决原有效理论（π介子有效理论）在短程/高能区失效、无法计算热共湮灭的问题。

Method: 构造弱耦合紫外完备：矢量媒介子规范QCD轻夸克味的重子流，并与参数化陪集 $SU(2)_D/U(1)_D$ 的暗π介子耦合；采用无反常实现即规范 $B_1+B_2-2B_3$ 组合；在此基础上计算包含共振媒介子效应的热共湮灭。

Result: 得到超出π介子有效理论适用范围的热共湮灭计算，并用LHC的boosted-dijet和monojet搜索、LEP数据、底偶素数据以及味观测对热靶进行检验；低动量转移下拓扑算符控制较重暗π介子的衰变（长寿命）。

Conclusion: 对撞机信号直接源自匹配拓扑门户所需的对称结构；未来高统计量Z工厂（如FCC-ee）可探测强子对撞机灵敏度恶化的低质量区，为拓扑门户提供互补检验。

Abstract: We study the phenomenology of the topological portal between QCD and pseudo-Nambu--Goldstone dark matter proposed in~\cite{Davighi:2024zip}. We construct a weakly coupled ultraviolet completion in which a vector mediator gauges a baryonic current of QCD for the light quark flavours, linking it to dark pions parametrizing the coset $SU(2)_D/U(1)_D$. As a concrete anomaly-free realisation, we gauge the leptophobic combination $B_1+B_2-2B_3$. This completion allows us to calculate thermal coannihilation beyond the regime of validity of the pion effective theory, including resonant mediator effects, and to test the resulting thermal target with boosted-dijet and monojet searches at the LHC, with LEP and bottomonium data, and with flavour observables. These collider signatures follow directly from the symmetry structure required by matching onto the topological portal. At low momentum transfer, the topological operator instead controls the decay of the heavier dark pion, which is long-lived. A future high-statistics $Z$ factory, such as FCC-ee, can probe the low-mass region where hadron-collider sensitivity deteriorates, providing complementary tests of the topological portal.

</details>


### [123] [EFT Approaches to Sommerfeld Enhancement and Bound States in Singular Potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10690)
*Arindam Bhattacharya,Rashmish K. Mishra,Tracy R. Slatyer*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文澄清了奇异势中Sommerfeld增强(SE)的起源，指出其可由短程正则化与紫外完备性相互作用产生，并在特定有效算符下即使弱耦合也出现SE与束缚态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程相互作用产生的Sommerfeld增强及其束缚态形成率是暗物质湮灭信号的重要非微扰输入；但奇异势下薛定谔方程常规边界条件失效，使SE的计算、物理起源及其与束缚态的关系变得模糊。

Method: 采用非相对论有效场论(NREFT)中的速度幂次计数框架，并结合位置空间短程正则化方法处理奇异势，以计算SE与束缚态；并以赝标量媒介子与有质量狄拉克暗物质的相互作用为示例。

Result: 若系统源于弱耦合的紫外完备理论，则不产生SE；而对于导数耦合的赝标量情形，相互作用由有效理论中的高维算符描述，当有效理论截断尺度与暗物质质量之间存在大层级时，弱耦合下也可出现SE与束缚态。该SE对短程势的紫外完备选择敏感，但即使紫外物理本身不产生SE，仍可能存在不可忽略的SE。

Conclusion: 奇异势中的SE源于红外与紫外物理的相互交织；其存在性和强度依赖于短程正则化及紫外完备性，而在高维算符与尺度层级条件下弱耦合亦可产生SE。

Abstract: The Sommerfeld enhancement (SE) from long-range interactions, and the related bound state formation rate, can be important non-perturbative inputs to dark matter (DM) annihilation signals. In the presence of singular interaction potentials, the conventional boundary conditions of the Schrödinger equation fail, obfuscating the computation of SE, its physical origin, and its relation to bound states in such potentials. In this work, we clarify the origin of SE in singular potentials in a two-fold manner: using the framework of velocity power counting in non-relativistic effective field theory (NREFT), and via position-space regularization of singular potentials at short distances to compute SE and bound states. We illustrate our findings through the case of pseudoscalar mediators interacting with massive Dirac DM. We find that when such a system arises from a UV-complete theory at weak coupling, no SE is generated. However, in the case of a derivatively coupled pseudoscalar, where the interaction is described by a higher-dimension operator in an effective theory, SE (and bound states) can occur for weak couplings if there is a large hierarchy between the cutoff scale of the effective theory and the dark matter mass. This SE is sensitive to the choice of the UV completion of the potential at short distances, but a non-negligible SE can persist even when the UV physics alone would not generate any SE; we elucidate the interplay of UV and IR physics in this case.

</details>


### [124] [Soft functions for generalised angularity event shapes at NNLO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10716)
*Emmet P. Byrne,Jonathan R. Gaunt*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模块化方法，用于计算一类趋向角度的双喷注事件形状在NLO和NNLO的软函数，并给出计算三个新事件形状族NNLO软函数的代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这类事件形状的因子化公式与角度仅软函数不同，NNLO软函数是实现NNLL'重求和唯一缺失的组成部分，因此需要发展通用计算方法。

Method: 采用模块化方法计算该事件形状类的软函数：其大部分结构可写成单个快度积分，剩余部分为三维数值积分，并提供相应计算代码。

Result: 给出了该事件形状类任意事件形状在NLO和NNLO的软函数计算方法与代码，并计算了三个新事件形状族的NNLO软函数以展示方法灵活性。

Conclusion: 该工作为NNLL'重求和提供关键组件，潜在应用包括未来e+e-对撞机强子化效应研究及存档数据再分析。

Abstract: We consider a class of dijet event shapes which tend to angularity in the forward limit. The factorisation formula for this class of event shapes differs from angularity only in the soft function. The NNLO soft function of an event shape in this class is therefore the only missing ingredient required for NNLL' resummation. We describe a modular approach to the calculation of the soft function for any event shape in this class, at NLO and NNLO. Much of the structure of the soft function can be written in terms of a single rapidity integral. The remainder can be computed as a three-dimensional numerical integral for which we provide a code. To demonstrate the flexibility of our approach, we compute the NNLO soft function for three new families of event shapes in this class. Potential applications include the investigation of hadronisation effects at future $e^+ e^-$ colliders, or through the re-analysis of archived data.

</details>


### [125] [A Colour-Casimir Adjacency Matrix Approach to Fully-Heavy Tetraquarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10822)
*M. Monemzadeh,N. Tazimi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于加权图谱理论，用完全图K₄的颜色加权邻接矩阵描述全重四夸克谱学，并以X(6900)、X(7100)、X(7200)标定参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为全重四夸克谱学提供一个现象学框架，通过图论方法刻画颜色自由度，并厘清颜色吸引与质量次序之间的物理关系。

Method: 将四个价部分子视为完全图K₄的顶点，边权由两个颜色单态双夸克-反双夸克通道（3̄⊗3与6⊗6̄）的颜色Casimir因子决定；物理态为两通道经单一混合角的相干叠加。不同于此前的拉普拉斯矩阵版本，直接对角化颜色加权邻接矩阵得到谱。

Result: 解析证明两种构造不等价，且拉普拉斯形式会反转颜色吸引与质量次序的预期关系。用X(6900)、X(7100)及初步的X(7200)定出α=0.740、γ=23.2 MeV、m_c=1.77 GeV；由于三参数由三输入固定，该标定不构成独立统计检验。预测全底基态为18.7–18.9 GeV，纯颜色模型对T_cc⁺欠束缚约93 MeV，指示存在超出紧凑四部分子颜色图的长程分子动力学。

Conclusion: 该框架将颜色结构抽象为图论问题，但作为静态模型不含自旋、轨道与衰变动力学；X(7200)具有初步性，且与CMS径向激发解释存在张力。

Abstract: We present a phenomenological framework for fully-heavy tetraquark spectroscopy based on the spectral theory of weighted graphs. The four valence partons are vertices of the complete graph $K_4$, with edge weights determined by colour-Casimir factors in the two colour-singlet diquark-antidiquark channels, $\bar{3}\otimes3$ and $6\otimes\bar{6}$. A physical state is described as a coherent mixture of these channels through one mixing angle. Unlike an earlier Laplacian-based version, we obtain the spectrum by directly diagonalising the colour-weighted adjacency matrix. We show analytically that the two constructions are inequivalent and that the Laplacian reverses the physically expected relation between colour attraction and mass ordering. Using $X(6900)$, $X(7100)$, and the tentative $X(7200)$ to determine the mixing angle, energy scale, and effective charm mass, we find $α=0.740$, $γ=23.2$ MeV, and $m_c=1.77$ GeV. Since three parameters are fixed by three inputs, this calibration is not an independent statistical test. The predictive content comes from applying the same parameters elsewhere: the all-bottom ground state is predicted at 18.7-18.9 GeV, while the colour-only model under-binds $T_{cc}^+$ by about 93 MeV. This discrepancy provides a quantitative indication of long-range molecular dynamics beyond a compact four-parton colour graph. We discuss the tentative nature of $X(7200)$, the tension with the CMS radial-excitation interpretation, and the limitations of a static model without spin, orbital, or decay dynamics.

</details>


### [126] [Generalized Chiral $U(1)_{B-L}$ with Inelastic Scalar Dark Matter for the LZ 248 keV Event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10827)
*Ranjeet Kumar,Hemant Kumar Prajapati*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在广义手征 $U(1)_{B-L}$ 框架下研究非弹性标量暗物质情景，用以解释 LZ 合作组报道的 $E_R\simeq248$ keV、仅背景显著性 $2.6\sigma$ 的高能核反冲事件。轻质量态作为暗物质候选者，通过 $Z'$ 玻色子发生离对角耦合，产生吸热非弹性散射，其运动学压低低速暗物质贡献而使高速尾部在高能核反冲中更为重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ 合作组近期报道了 $E_R\simeq248$ keV 的高能核反冲事件，其仅背景显著性为 $2.6\sigma$，这为超越传统弹性图像的暗物质散射提供了可能窗口。作者希望探讨非弹性暗物质散射能否解释该观测，并研究其与现有实验约束及对撞机探测的互补性。

Method: 在广义手征 $U(1)_{B-L}$ 框架下构建非弹性标量暗物质模型：对称性破缺后暗扇区标量产生两个近简并态，具有小质量劈裂；较轻态作为暗物质候选者，与 $Z'$ 玻色子发生离对角耦合，导致吸热非弹性散射。作者分析相关散射运动学，并识别与观测遗迹丰度及当前实验约束相符的参数空间。

Result: 非弹性散射的运动学压低低速暗物质的贡献，同时使高速尾部对高能核反冲更为重要。作者确定了与观测遗迹丰度及当前实验约束一致的相关参数空间，并讨论了与近期 LZ 观测相关的非弹性散射运动学，以及相关 $Z'$ 玻色子的互补对撞机探测前景。

Conclusion: 在广义手征 $U(1)_{B-L}$ 框架下的非弹性标量暗物质情景，可通过吸热非弹性散射及其运动学特性，为 LZ 高能核反冲事件提供一种可能的解释路径，并与遗迹丰度、实验约束及 $Z'$ 对撞机探测形成互补检验。

Abstract: The high-energy nuclear recoil event with $E_R\simeq248$ keV recently reported by the LZ collaboration, with a background-only significance of $2.6σ$, offers an intriguing window into dark matter scattering beyond the conventional elastic picture. We study an inelastic scalar dark matter scenario within a generalized chiral $U(1)_{B-L}$ framework. After symmetry breaking, the dark sector scalar gives rise to two nearly degenerate states with a small mass splitting. The lighter state serves as the dark matter candidate and couples off-diagonally to the $Z'$ boson, leading to endothermic inelastic scattering. The resulting kinematics suppress the contribution from low-velocity dark matter while making the high-velocity tail increasingly relevant for nuclear recoils at higher energies. We identify the parameter space consistent with the observed relic abundance and current experimental constraints, and discuss the inelastic scattering kinematics relevant to the recent LZ observation, together with the complementary collider prospects for the associated $Z'$ boson.

</details>


### [127] [Glueball interactions from the colour Van der Waals potential](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10841)
*Juan Cánovas Cúneo,Felipe J. Llanes-Estrada*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在组分胶子框架下研究纯Yang-Mills理论中胶球-胶球相互作用，发现色Van der Waals力在约0.66–1.9 fm范围内相关，且由其线性势部分导出的贡献与库仑部分近乎抵消，故该相互作用远弱于仅由库仑部分得到的London力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯Yang-Mills理论存在大质量间隙，导致胶球间Van der Waals相互作用相对轻夸克QCD强子中由π介子主导的Yukawa交换更为重要，因此有必要在组分胶子图像下系统考察胶球-胶球相互作用的性质与强度。

Method: 采用组分胶子方法，沿量子London-Eisenschitz-Wang力的传统思路计算色Van der Waals势；以Cornell势（库仑项加线性项）作为色荷间的微观相互作用，分别评估其库仑部分与线性部分对Van der Waals相互作用的贡献。

Result: 色Van der Waals势在约0.66 fm（胶球接触时）至1.9 fm（中间态色极化胶球间虚弦张力失效时）的距离范围内是相关相互作用。对基态胶球，由线性势部分导出的Van der Waals相互作用与库仑部分的贡献近乎抵消，说明该相互作用虽具一定量级并可在特定参数下满足格点数据，但远弱于仅由库仑部分得到的London力。

Conclusion: 胶球间Van der Waals相互作用显著弱于库仑型London力，这提示胶球有效理论应超越dilaton型方法，或可采用Van der Waals有效场论的某种变体。

Abstract: We examine pure Yang-Mills theory glueball-glueball interactions in constituent-gluon approaches. Because of the large mass gap, Van der Waals interactions are relatively more significant than in light-quark QCD hadrons where the pion gives rise to strong Yukawa exchanges. We find that the colour Van der Waals potential, computed along the traditional lines of the quantum London-Eisenschitz-Wang force, is a relevant interaction at distances between about 0.66 fm (when the glueballs are in contact) to 1.9 fm (when the virtual string tension among the colour-polarized glueballs in the intermediate state breaks down). In employing a Cornell potential as the microscopic one among colour charges, we find that the Van der Waals interaction derived from the linear potential part closely cancels that of the Coulomb one for the ground state glueballs. This means that the interaction, while sizeable and capable of saturating the lattice data depending on parameters, is far weaker than the London force derived from the Coulombic part alone. (This suggests that one should move effective theories for glueballs beyond dilaton-type approaches and perhaps deploy some variant of Van der Waals Effective Field Theory.)

</details>


### [128] [Unitarity dressing of the dynamical gluon mass scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10903)
*T. V. Iser,V. Li,E. G. S. Luna*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 研究s道幺正性对由高能弹性pp散射提取的动力学胶子质量标度的影响：Born层面给出299–422 MeV，嵌入eikonal与U矩阵后升至749–1101 MeV，增强因子约2.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性pp散射常被用于提取动力学胶子质量标度，但通常忽略s道幺正性（多交换）对红外标度的重整作用，导致提取值可能被系统性低估。

Method: 以Regge化的Landshoff–Nachtmann双胶子交换为基本输入，将软Pomeron表示为色单态动力学质量胶子对；先在Born层面用对数解与幂律解提取质量，再将该输入嵌入eikonal方案和U矩阵幺正化方案，并对比ATLAS–TOTEM数据、质量跑动及不同幺正化约定。

Result: Born层面给出m_g=299–422 MeV；eikonal与U矩阵框架下优选值为749–1101 MeV；增强约2.5倍，且对数据选择、胶子质量跑动及幺正化方案稳健。

Conclusion: 该移动源于基本双胶子核与物理碰撞参数剖面之间的非线性映射；由弹性散射推断的标度是幺正化修饰后的红外标度，由非微扰胶子传播子与多次交换动力学共同决定。

Abstract: We study the effect of $s$-channel unitarity on the dynamical gluon mass scale, $m_g$, extracted from high-energy elastic $pp$ scattering. The elementary input is a Reggeized Landshoff--Nachtmann two-gluon exchange, in which the soft Pomeron is represented by a color-singlet pair of dynamically massive gluons. At Born level, the logarithmic and power-law mass solutions give $m_g=299$--$422~\MeV$, in the usual phenomenological range. When the same input is embedded in the eikonal and $U$-matrix schemes, the preferred values move to $m_g=749$--$1101~\MeV$. The enhancement, by a factor close to $2.5$, is stable against the ATLAS--TOTEM data choice, the running of the gluon mass, and the unitarization prescription. We trace this shift to the nonlinear mapping between the elementary two-gluon kernel and the physical impact-parameter profile. The scale inferred from elastic scattering is therefore a unitarity-dressed infrared scale, fixed jointly by the nonperturbative gluon propagator and by multiple-exchange dynamics.

</details>


### [129] [Axionic Wormholes in Metric-Affine Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11010)
*Shonosuke Takeshita,Naoki Yoshioka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在度量-仿射引力框架下，轴子虫洞可作为显式U(1)破缺源；Holst项与Nieh–Yan项的非最小耦合增强虫洞欧氏作用量，从而缓解轴子质量问题，并可与暴胀约束兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子被认为是解决强CP问题的有力候选，但该方案要求整体U(1)对称性保持极高精度。量子引力效应通常会显式破坏整体对称性，由此产生"轴子质量问题"，因此需要寻找能抑制这种破缺的机制。

Method: 在度量-仿射引力（Metric-Affine Gravity）框架中研究轴子虫洞，该框架包含时空挠率与非度规性，允许Holst项、Nieh–Yan项等额外曲率型与拓扑项。分析这些项与轴子场的非最小耦合对虫洞动力学及欧氏虫洞作用量的影响，并扫描参数空间以考察两个耦合同时存在时的情形，同时检验与暴胀约束的相容性。

Result: 非最小耦合Holst项与Nieh–Yan项会修正虫洞动力学，并增大欧氏虫洞作用量，从而压制U(1)破缺、缓解轴子质量问题。当两个耦合同时存在时，可行的参数空间进一步扩大。研究还给出了若干代表性参数区域，使得轴子质量问题的缓解与暴胀约束相容。

Conclusion: 度量-仿射引力中的轴子虫洞为缓解轴子质量问题提供了新途径，其额外挠率与非度规耦合不仅增强了对U(1)破缺的抑制，还拓宽了可行参数空间，并能与暴胀观测约束同时满足。

Abstract: The axion is a promising candidate for solving the strong CP problem. To solve this problem, the global U(1) symmetry must be preserved to a high degree of accuracy. However, it is well known that global symmetries are explicitly violated by quantum gravity effects, giving rise to what is referred to as the axion quality problem. In this paper, we investigate axionic wormholes as a source of explicit U(1) violation in Metric-Affine Gravity. This framework allows for spacetime torsion and non-metricity, which accommodate additional curvature-like and topological terms, such as the Holst and Nieh--Yan terms, that are absent from the metric and Palatini formalisms. We show that non-minimal couplings to these terms modify the wormhole dynamics and enhance the Euclidean wormhole action, thereby alleviating the axion quality problem. We also find that the viable parameter space is enlarged when two of these couplings are simultaneously present. We further identify representative parameter regions where the alleviation of the axion quality problem is compatible with inflationary constraints.

</details>


### [130] [Qubit-Qutrit Quantum Tomography of hadronic $Λφ$ and $ΛK^{\ast 0}$ systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11151)
*Veronica Kanachova,Feng Liu,Zhoudunming Tu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出qubit-qutrit量子层析（QQQT）技术，用于自旋-1/2⊗1系统，并从不完全密度矩阵建立纠缠认证的精确判据，将其应用于Λφ和ΛK*0体系以探测非微扰QCD强子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将量子信息观测量推广到不等自旋的强子末态面临根本挑战：qubit-qutrit系统的6×6密度矩阵含35个独立自旋参数，而ΛV（V=φ或K*0）衰变由于强衰变隐藏的矢量极化只能获取23个，需要新方法处理这种不完整信息。

Method: 针对自旋-1/2⊗1系统构建qubit-qutrit量子层析（QQQT）技术，并基于该不完全密度矩阵建立纠缠认证的精确判据。

Result: QQQT可实现Λφ和ΛK*0体系的量子层析，相比ΛΛbar系统，提供了探测非微扰QCD强子化的新途径，并可直接比较从真空产生并强子化为重子或矢量介子的纠缠夸克对的自旋演化。

Conclusion: QQQT技术克服了不等自旋末态中密度矩阵信息不完整的难题，为利用ΛV体系研究非微扰QCD强子化提供了新的量子信息探针。

Abstract: Quantum-information observables have emerged in recent years as new tools in nuclear and particle physics, from entanglement in top-quark pairs to spin correlations in $Λ\barΛ$ production. Extending these studies to unequal-spin hadronic final states poses a fundamental challenge: the $6\times6$ density matrix of a qubit-qutrit system contains 35 independent spin parameters, but the decays of $ΛV$ pairs, with $V=φ$ or $K^{*0}$, provide access to only 23 due to the hidden vector polarization from the strong decay. In this Letter, we formulate a qubit-qutrit quantum tomography (QQQT) technique for these spin-$\tfrac{1}{2}\otimes1$ systems and establish exact criteria for entanglement certification from the \textit{incomplete} density matrix. Compared with the $Λ\barΛ$ system, QQQT of $Λφ$ and $ΛK^{*0}$ provides a new probe of nonperturbative QCD hadronization, enabling a direct comparison of the spin evolution of entangled quark pairs produced from the vacuum as they hadronize into a baryon or a vector meson.

</details>


### [131] [Twist decomposition of exclusive heavy meson production cross sections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11179)
*Mariusz Sadzikowski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在领头对数1/x BFKL框架下研究γ*p过程中独家重矢量介子电产生与光产生总截面的twist分解，计算矢量介子碰撞因子的Mellin变换，发现低x区域高阶twist贡献被强烈压低，并讨论了核靶可能的高阶twist增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独家重矢量介子产生是探测质子内部胶子分布和小x物理的重要过程；明确twist结构中高阶twist的贡献大小，对判断该过程的适用性和提取胶子信息的可靠性具有关键意义。

Method: 在领头对数1/x的BFKL形式主义下，对γ*p过程中独家重矢量介子电产生和光产生总截面进行twist分解，并计算矢量介子碰撞因子（impact factors）的Mellin变换。

Result: 计算得到矢量介子碰撞因子的Mellin变换；结果显示在低x运动学区域，高阶twist贡献被强烈压低。

Conclusion: 低x区域高阶twist效应可忽略，领头twist近似有效；但核靶情形下高阶twist效应可能存在增强，值得进一步讨论。

Abstract: We study the twist decomposition of the total cross sections for exclusive heavy vector meson electroproduction and photoproduction in the $γ^\ast p$ processes, within the leading logarithmic $1/x$ BFKL formalism. The Mellin transforms of the impact factors of the vector meson are calculated. We show that the higher twist contributions are strongly suppressed in the low-$x$ kinematical regime. Possible enhancement of the higher twists effects for nuclei targets is discussed.

</details>


### [132] [Doubling down on proton structure: what double parton scattering can and cannot tell us about partonic separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11196)
*Andrew Fowlie,Arthur Moraes,Hao Yang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在双部分子散射的因子化假设下，有效截面σ_eff可给出质子内部分子横向间距的严格下界⟨r²⟩ ≥ 4σ_eff/(9π)，但无法给出通用上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双部分子散射是探测质子结构的独特手段，通常用有效截面σ_eff表征。作者希望在不假定横向分布具体参数形式的前提下，厘清σ_eff究竟能揭示质子的哪些横向结构信息，尤其是部分子间的横向间距r。

Method: 在双部分子散射截面满足因子化假设的框架内，不对横向轮廓（transverse profile）假设任何特定参数形式，通过解析推导研究σ_eff与横向部分子间距⟨r²⟩之间的数学关系，并构造显式反例检验既有上界。

Result: 论文推导出横向部分子间距的严格下界：⟨r²⟩ ≥ 4σ_eff/(9π)；并通过显式反例证明先前提出的上界并不成立。

Conclusion: 在有效截面参数化的双部分子散射框架内，σ_eff能提供部分子间距的严格下界，但无法提供通用上界。

Abstract: Double parton scattering provides a unique probe of proton structure and is commonly characterized by the effective cross section $σ_\mathrm{eff}$. We examine, under the factorized ansatz for the double-parton-scattering cross section and without assuming any specific parametric form for the transverse profile, what the effective cross section can reveal about the transverse separation, $r$, between partons in the proton. We derive a sharp lower bound on the transverse partonic separation, $\langle r^2\rangle \ge 4σ_\mathrm{eff}/(9π)$, and demonstrate through explicit counterexamples that previously posited upper bounds do not hold. Our results establish, within the effective-cross-section parametrization of double parton scattering framework, that $σ_\mathrm{eff}$ can provide a sharp lower bound on partonic separation but cannot provide a general upper bound.

</details>


### [133] [Higgsino dark matter in the Starobinsky supergravity with the MSSM in light of the LUX-ZEPLIN event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11241)
*Daniel Frolovsky,Sergei V. Ketov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在最小超对称标准模型与Starobinsky超引力耦合框架下，可实现质量约1 TeV的近乎纯higgsino暗物质候选者，且其粒子谱与宇宙暴胀观测量相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期LUX-ZEPLIN 248 keV核反冲事件重新激发了学界对higgsino暗物质的兴趣，本文旨在该动机下构建一个与观测相符的higgsino暗物质模型。

Method: 将最小超对称标准模型与Starobinsky超引力耦合，通过超对称破缺的引力传递及随后的重整化群演化，把粒子谱（希格斯玻色子质量、higgsino质量及其劈裂）与宇宙暴胀观测量联系起来。

Result: 模型预测的希格斯玻色子质量在较宽的bino诱导质量劈裂范围内与测量值在给定不确定度内一致；wino诱导的劈裂使希格斯质量高出几个GeV，因此被排除。

Conclusion: 该框架可给出与观测相符的约1 TeV近乎纯higgsino暗物质，bino诱导劈裂可行而wino诱导劈裂被排除。

Abstract: We realize a nearly pure higgsino dark matter candidate with mass of about 1 TeV in the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model coupled to the Starobinsky supergravity. The recent LUX-ZEPLIN 248 keV nuclear-recoil event has renewed interest in higgsino dark matter. In this framework, the particle spectrum, including the Higgs boson mass, the higgsino mass and its splitting, is connected to cosmic inflation observables via gravitational mediation of supersymmetry breaking and subsequent renormalization group evolution. We show that the Higgs boson mass predicted by the model agrees with the measured value within the quoted uncertainties for a broad range of bino-induced mass splittings. Wino-induced splitting leads to a Higgs mass several GeV higher and is therefore excluded.

</details>


### [134] [Relocating the SIMP Miracle in the Axion Portal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11254)
*Nakorn Thongyoi,Chakrit Pongkitivanichkul*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 研究轴子门下的SIMP暗物质情景：动能接触项被亚GeV ALP束缚排除，暗区在自身温度冻结，遗迹丰度转而预言门户耦合；接触项驱动ππ→aa极快转换，排除最小实现，但保留的实现将预言力从自相互作用转移到flavon能标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIMP情景假定暗物质为赝Nambu-Goldstone玻色子，其丰度由3→2 Wess-Zumino-Witten过程决定，其中动能接触项是关键。作者想在轴子门户（axion portal）中检验该情景是否自洽，因为该接触项可能受亚GeV轴子类粒子（ALP）实验束缚限制而被排除。

Method: 在轴子门户框架下分析SIMP机制，检验动能接触项与ALP束缚的一致性；考虑暗区在自身温度下冻结；检查门户算符的Hermitian性与ALP场宇称，判断是否存在三线性ALP-π介子耦合；计算接触项驱动的ππ→aa转换速率并与三线性估计对比；通过匹配观测丰度确定flavon真空期望值及暗能标。

Result: 动能接触项被亚GeV ALP束缚排除；暗区在自身温度冻结，遗迹丰度不再固定自相互作用截面而改为预言门户耦合；门户算符为Hermitian且ALP场偶，无三线性ALP-π耦合，接触项驱动ππ→aa无抵消，转换速率比三线性估计快四个数量级；排除暗凝聚独自产生ALP质量的最小实现；幸存实现使ALP略重于暗π介子；匹配丰度得flavon VEV V_φ≈1.1×10^10 GeV，暗能标留有超过一个数量级的空间；K^+→π^+a要求flavon仅给轻子电荷；预言暗物质自相互作用为0.20 cm²/g（暗π质量140 MeV），窗口为82–169 MeV。

Conclusion: SIMP框架的预言能力并未丧失，而是从自相互作用转移到flavon能标上。

Abstract: In the strongly interacting massive particle (SIMP) scenario, dark matter is a pseudo-Nambu--Goldstone boson whose abundance is set by a three-to-two Wess--Zumino--Witten process. We study this scenario in an axion portal and find that the kinetic contact the canonical mechanism assumes is excluded by bounds on sub-GeV axion-like particles (ALPs). The dark sector then freezes out at its own temperature, so the relic abundance no longer fixes the self-interaction cross section but predicts the portal coupling instead. Moreover, the portal operator is Hermitian and even in the ALP field, so no trilinear ALP--pion coupling arises. The contact term then drives $ππ\to aa$ with nothing to cancel against it, and we find the conversion four orders of magnitude faster than a trilinear estimate gives. This disfavours the minimal realisation in which the dark condensate alone generates the ALP mass. The realisation that survives instead makes the ALP slightly heavier than the dark pion. Matching the observed abundance then fixes the flavon vacuum expectation value at $V_φ\simeq 1.1\times10^{10} \ {\rm GeV}$ and leaves the dark scale open over more than an order of magnitude. The decay $K^+\toπ^+a$ requires the flavon to charge the leptons alone. The model then predicts a dark matter self-interaction of $0.20\,{\rm cm^2/g}$ at a dark pion mass of $140\ {\rm MeV}$, in a window running from $82$ to $169 \ {\rm MeV}$. The predictive power of the SIMP framework is therefore not lost but relocated, from the self-interaction to the flavon scale.

</details>


### [135] [Broad p-Wave Sommerfeld Resonances From Dimensional Deconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11305)
*Nobuki Yoshimatsu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究维度解构对长程力的影响，发现五维AdS时空的维度解构会有效产生平方反比势，从而无需精细调节即可自然产生宽泛的p波Sommerfeld共振，并将该机制应用于暗物质现象学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究维度解构是否能定性改变长程力，并借此为暗物质现象学寻找新的理论机制。

Method: 对五维AdS时空进行维度解构（deconstruction），推导其有效势结构；分析该有效势所导致的Sommerfeld共振特征；进而将机制应用于暗物质现象学。

Result: 五维AdS时空的维度解构有效生成平方反比势，自然导致宽泛（broad）的p波Sommerfeld共振，且无需精细调节。

Conclusion: 维度解构可定性改变长程力，其产生的平方反比势为暗物质现象学提供了一种无需精细调节的p波共振增强机制。

Abstract: We investigate whether dimensional deconstruction can qualitatively modify long-range forces. We show that the deconstruction of a five-dimensional AdS spacetime effectively generates an inverse-square potential, which naturally gives rise to broad p-wave Sommerfeld resonances without fine-tuning. We then apply this mechanism to dark matter phenomenology.

</details>


### [136] [Connecting Vector-like Muons, pNGB Dark Matter and Electroweak Phase Transition through Collider and Gravitational Wave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11386)
*Jaydeb Das,Saurabh Niyogi,Amir Subba*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了包含两个复标量单态S2、S3和μ子亲和的矢量类轻子ψ的扩展标准模型，实现了有效单组分pNGB暗物质场景，并探讨了暗物质探测、μ子对撞机探测与一阶电弱相变引力波信号的互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索pNGB暗物质与μ子物理的关联，并考察暗物质直接探测、μ子对撞机间接探测以及电弱相变引力波三种手段之间的互补性。

Method: 构建带有Z2和Z3分立对称性的双复标量单态扩展标准模型；通过软破缺全局U(1)对称性产生暗物质质量；利用矢量类μ子使第二赝标量不稳定；计算单圈暗物质-核子散射截面；对3 TeVμ子对撞机进行多元分析；寻找具有强一阶电弱相变和成功气泡成核的基准点。

Result: pNGB特性强烈压低树级自旋无关直接探测截面，可行参数区域能重现观测到的遗迹丰度并满足LHC单喷注限制；单圈DM-核子散射截面低于当前实验上限但有望被未来直接探测实验观测到；多TeVμ子对撞机上标量介导的t沟道过程可显著增强矢量类μ子对产生；识别出具有强一阶电弱相变、可产生可观测随机引力波信号的可行基准点。

Conclusion: 该模型展示了暗物质搜寻、μ子对撞机探测和强一阶电弱相变三者的互补性，产生的引力波信号为扩展标量扇区提供了额外探测手段。

Abstract: We study an extension of the Standard Model with two complex scalar singlets, $S_2$ and $S_3$, charged under $\mathbb{Z}_2$ and $\mathbb{Z}_3$, respectively, and a muon-philic vector-like lepton $ψ$ carrying the same $\mathbb{Z}_3$ charge as $S_3$. The pseudoscalar associated with the $\mathbb{Z}_2$ sector remains stable due to the CP symmetry of the potential and serves as the dark matter (DM) candidate, with its mass generated through the corresponding soft breaking of the global $U(1)$ symmetry. The vector-like muon couples to the $\mathbb{Z}_3$ scalar and renders the second pseudoscalar unstable, thereby realizing an effectively single-component pNGB DM scenario. The pNGB nature strongly suppresses the tree-level spin-independent direct-detection cross section, while viable parameter regions reproduce the observed relic abundance and satisfy LHC monojet constraints. We further compute the one-loop contribution to DM-nucleon scattering and find that the resulting cross section remains below current experimental limits while being potentially accessible to future direct-detection experiments. At a multi-TeV muon collider, scalar mediated $ t$-channel processes can significantly enhance vector-like-muon pair production. We perform a detailed multivariate analysis for a future muon collider at 3 TeV center-of-mass energy. We also identify viable benchmark points exhibiting strong first-order electroweak phase transitions (SFOEWPT) with successful bubble nucleation, which can generate potentially observable stochastic gravitational wave (GW) signals. These results highlight the complementarity of dark matter searches, muon-collider probes, and SFOEWPT, with the resulting GW signals providing an additional probe of the extended scalar sector.

</details>


### [137] [Net-baryon number distributions and the QCD phase diagram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11483)
*R. N. Rogalyov,N. V. Gerasimeniuk*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文在大正则系综框架下分析STAR合作组的净质子多重数分布，首次提出一种直接从该分布确定重子化学势μ_B的方法，所得μ_B与统计热模型及HRG模型拟合结果一致，并提出重火球热化判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从实验测量的净质子多重数分布中直接、快速地提取重子化学势μ_B，并借此判断重核碰撞中产生的火球是否达到热化。

Method: 采用大正则系综方法处理STAR合作组的净质子多重数分布，首次应用一种从该分布直接求解重子化学势μ_B的方法，并将结果与统计热模型及强子共振气体（HRG）模型拟合结果进行对比。

Result: 由新方法得到的μ_B值与此前用统计热模型确定的值以及基于HRG模型拟合的结果均保持一致。

Conclusion: 该直接提取μ_B的方法可行且结果自洽，并据此提出了判断重核碰撞火球热化的判据。

Abstract: Net-proton multiplicity distributions obtained by the STAR collaboration are analyzed in the grand canonical approach. A method of finding the baryon chemical potential $μ_B$ immediately from such distributions is applied for the first time. The obtained values of $μ_B$ are consistent both with those determined earlier using the statistical thermal model and with the results of the fit based on the Hadron Resonance Gas (HRG) model. We propose a criterion of thermalization of fireballs produced in heavy-nuclei collisions.

</details>


### [138] [Mathematical inverse problem for the world data inference of the parton distribution functions of the proton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11896)
*Henri Hänninen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 将质子部分子分布函数(PDF)的推断问题重新表述为线性张量重建逆问题，用耦合线性积分方程组替代传统参数拟合，提出无模型偏倚的PDF提取原理性方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PDF全局分析依赖模型参数拟合，会引入参数化偏倚。作者希望借助数学逆问题的"间接测量"思想，实现无模型偏倚的PDF提取并减少不确定性估计中的偏倚。

Method: 利用微扰QCD与全局分析中积分方程的数学结构，将全局分析问题表述为耦合线性积分方程组，并构建原理验证性(proof-of-principle)求解方法。为此系统梳理了各类深度非弹性散射过程的次领头阶(NLO)结果，包括包容虚光子、中性流、带电流、重味产生以及中微子深度非弹性轻子-质子散射。

Result: 证明了从深度非弹性散射数据约束质子PDF的推断问题可表述为线性张量重建逆问题，即无需拟合模型参数，而是通过求解耦合线性泛函积分方程组来完成重建。

Conclusion: 该数学推断方法为从全球深度非弹性散射数据中实现无模型偏倚的质子PDF提取开辟了路径，可在遵循微扰QCD与全局分析既定范式的前提下，获得偏倚减少的不确定性估计。

Abstract: We show that the inference problem of constraining the parton distribution functions of the proton from deeply inelastic scattering data can be formulated as a linear tensor reconstruction inverse problem. This means that instead of fitting model parameters to the world data, a reconstructive approach to solve a system of coupled linear functional integral equations can be formulated. We leverage the mathematical structures of the integral equations defined by perturbative QCD and global analysis to pose the problem of global analysis as a coupled system of linear integral equations, and construct a proof-of-principle methodology to solve it. To formulate this approach concretely, we review all next-to-leading order accuracy results for inclusive virtual photon, neutral current, charged current, heavy flavor production, and neutrino deep-inelastic lepton--proton scattering. This mathematical methodology of inference opens a path towards a model-bias-free extraction of the proton PDFs from the world data of deeply inelastic scattering in the spirit of indirect measurement employed in mathematical inverse problems, including robust estimation of uncertainties with reduced bias from model parametrization, while closely adhering to the established paradigm of perturbative QCD and global analysis.

</details>


### [139] [Cosmic ray boosted dark matter with momentum dependent interactions can explain the LZ 248 keV event](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11600)
*Matti Heikinheimo,Niklas Zimmermann*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: LZ实验观测到248 keV单例核反冲事件且无预期本底，本文提出该事件可能源于宇宙线加速的暗物质，并通过非相对论有效理论分析其反冲谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LZ实验报告了一个能量高达248 keV的单例核反冲事件，而该能量区间预期无本底事件，因此需要解释该异常信号的物理来源。

Method: 在非相对论有效理论（NREFT）框架下，分析宇宙线加速暗物质（CRDM）产生的预期核反冲谱，考察各有效算符对谱形的贡献。

Result: 动量依赖型算符如O6和O10能够产生峰值位于观测能量（248 keV）处的反冲谱，可解释该事件。

Conclusion: 高速度宇宙线加速暗物质可解释LZ观测到的高能核反冲单例事件，动量依赖算符O6和O10尤其能再现观测谱形。

Abstract: The LZ experiment has recently reported an observation of a single event with characteristics of a nuclear recoil at a high recoil energy of 248 keV, where no background events are expected. We show that such nuclear recoil event could be caused by high velocity dark matter particles originating from interactions with energetic cosmic rays. We analyze the expected recoil spectrum due to cosmic ray boosted dark matter within the non-relativistic effective theory formalism, and show that momentum-dependent operators, such as $\mathcal{O}_6$ and $\mathcal{O}_{10}$, can produce a spectrum that peaks at the observed energy.

</details>


### [140] [Collinear fragmentation of pseudoscalar quarkonia from NLO NRQCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11604)
*Francesco Giovanni Celiberto,Francesca Lonigro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NRFF1.0——一套公开可用的赝标量夸克偶素共线碎裂函数集，基于HF-NRevo演化方案，由NRQCD给出初始标度输入，为中等及大横动量区重味强子产生提供精确基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重夸克偶素产生同时涉及微扰与非微扰QCD动力学，需要能统一描述从重夸克对产生到束缚态形成过程的理论工具，并为喷注内夸克偶素、喷注淬火等研究提供精确基线。

Method: 构建NRFF1.0共线碎裂函数集：以NRQCD为理论框架计算初始标度处的碎裂函数输入，再通过重味非相对论演化方案（HF-NRevo）进行演化，显式处理各部分子道与重味阈值。

Result: 给出了一套公开可用的赝标量夸克偶素共线碎裂函数集，可在中高横动量区描述重强子形成的领头幂碎裂，并刻画部分子层级与喷注结构。

Conclusion: NRFF1.0框架适用于研究喷注内夸克偶素碎裂、喷注淬火敏感性、能量损失机制以及夸克胶子等离子体中介质修正碎裂模式的出现。

Abstract: We discuss a new family of publicly available collinear fragmentation functions (FFs) for pseudoscalar quarkonia, the NRFF1.0 set. It builds upon the Heavy-Flavor Non-Relativistic evolution (HF-NRevo) scheme, designed to describe heavy-hadron formation through leading-power fragmentation at moderate and large transverse momentum. Heavy-quarkonium production naturally involves both perturbative and non-perturbative QCD dynamics, from the production of the heavy $Q\bar{Q}$ pair to its formation into a physical bound state. Within NRFF1.0, Non-Relativistic Quantum Chromodynamics (NRQCD) provides the theoretical framework for calculating the FF inputs at the initial scale, which are subsequently evolved through the HF-NRevo scheme. This setup provides a precision baseline for investigating the underlying partonic hierarchy and jet structure across the moderate- to high-transverse-momentum regime.
